# Database

For developers and database administrators: what every table holds, the rules the database itself keeps, and the catalogues of business types, modules and permission codes.

PostgreSQL 15 or newer (built and tested on 18). One database holds the whole platform; `DB_NAME` names it (`business_platform_db` in `backend/.env.example`). The schema is defined **only** by the SQL files in `backend/migrations/`, and this document describes it as the newest of them leaves it.

| Part | Tables | Scoped by |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [Platform and access (restaurant module)](#platform-and-access) | restaurants, outlets, users, roles, permissions, sign-in and approvals | `restaurant_id` |
| [Restaurant module](#restaurant-module) | settings, menu, tables, orders, kitchen, bills and payments, cash, guests, stock and purchasing, staff and pay | `restaurant_id`, and `outlet_id` where a branch matters |
| [Business platform](#business-platform) | platform catalogue, business access, business core, shared core, one extension group per business type, tasks, issues & projects, GST rates by date, personal data, and an owner's GST thresholds (both engines) | `business_id` on every row, with composite foreign keys (an owner's own settings by `user_id`; the platform's figures by none) |
| [Live updates](#live-updates) | no tables: the triggers that announce each committed change to the live screens, for both engines | the restaurant or business named in each notification |
| [Catalogues](#business-types) | business types, platform module codes, business and restaurant permission codes | — |

**Business platform sections:** [Platform](#platform) · [Identity and access](#identity-and-access) · [Business core](#business-core) · [Shared core](#shared-core) · [Quotations](#quotations) · [Licences](#licences) · [Retail](#retail) · [Wholesale](#wholesale) · [Fashion](#fashion) · [Electronics](#electronics) · [Salon](#salon) · [Weight](#weight) · [Pharmacy, clinic & lab](#pharmacy-clinic--lab) · [Hardware](#hardware) · [Seeds, fertilisers & pesticides](#seeds-fertilisers--pesticides) · [Optical shops](#optical-shops) · [Vehicle service & auto parts](#vehicle-service--auto-parts) · [Gyms, studios & coaching](#gyms-studios--coaching) · [Laundry & dry cleaning](#laundry--dry-cleaning) · [Jewellery & gold](#jewellery--gold) · [Rentals & hire](#rentals--hire) · [Fuel stations](#fuel-stations) · [Hotels, lodges & guest houses](#hotels-lodges--guest-houses) · [Tasks, issues & projects](#tasks-issues--projects) · [GST rates by date](#gst-rates-by-date) · [Live updates](#live-updates) · [Personal data](#personal-data) · [Owner's GST thresholds](#owners-gst-thresholds). **Catalogues:** [Business types](#business-types) · [Platform module codes](#platform-module-codes) · [Business permission codes](#business-permission-codes) · [Restaurant permission codes](#restaurant-permission-codes).

The business platform's design (its five layers, the rules every table follows, and why) is explained in [BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md). This file gives the table-by-table reference and links there instead of repeating it. A restaurant also appears on the platform as one `businesses` row whose `legacy_restaurant_id` points at it; its own tables are unchanged.

## Migrations

Tables are created and changed only by the migration files in `backend/migrations/`, each with an `-- Up Migration` and a `-- Down Migration` half; the migration tool records the files it has run in `pgmigrations`, which is never edited by hand. The commands, how to add a migration, and the rules (never change tables by hand, never edit a migration that has been applied anywhere, always write the Down half) are in [ARCHITECTURE.md › Add a migration](ARCHITECTURE.md#85-add-a-migration).

## Conventions used by every table

| Column / object | Meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `id BIGINT GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY` | Primary key, generated by the database (ids cannot be supplied). |
| `restaurant_id` / `business_id` | The tenant that owns the row. Restaurant queries filter by the signed-in user's restaurant; business tables carry `business_id` in their composite keys, so a row can only point at rows of the same business. |
| `outlet_id` | The restaurant branch, where the row belongs to one. `NULL` usually means "the whole restaurant" (or the main store). |
| `created_at`, `updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ` | Kept by the database; `updated_at` by a trigger. |
| `created_by`, `updated_by` → `users` | Who made or last changed the row. |
| `status` + `CHECK` | The record's state, limited to listed values. |
| Money | `NUMERIC(12,2)` or wider; never floating point. Quantities `NUMERIC(14,3)` or wider. |

**`set_updated_at()`** — created in the first migration and attached to almost
every table as `trg_<table>_updated_at BEFORE UPDATE`, it sets
`updated_at = NOW()`.

**Rules in the database, not only in code.** Wherever losing money or stock
would follow from a bug, the rule is enforced by the database too: CHECK
constraints, partial unique indexes (for example one open business day per
outlet), and triggers that refuse an edit of a posted record or keep running
balances. Each table below lists those rules. A trigger's refusal is a
sentence written for people: the business platform's routes return it as a
409 ([API.md › Business Admin conventions](API.md#business-admin-conventions)),
and the restaurant services check the same rules before writing, so there the
database's refusal is the backstop (a menu rule that still reaches it answers
400, a feature switch 409). Any other trigger's refusal (23514 naming no
constraint) answers 409 with its sentence on every route, as the restaurant's
cash limit and buyer's PAN do (`middlewares/errorHandler.js`).

## Platform and access

### Tenants

#### `restaurants`
Purpose: the tenant. Every restaurant-module row hangs off one restaurant.
Key columns: `name` (varchar 150), `code` (varchar 30, globally unique short code; also asked for when signing in by mobile across restaurants), `contact`, `email`, `address`, `status` (`active` / `inactive` / `suspended`), `format` (`restaurant` / `cafe` / `bakery` / `cloud_kitchen`, default `restaurant`: full service, counter first, production and pre-orders, or delivery only). Choosing a format also sets the POS order types and tokens in `service_settings` and switches features once; that is code (`restaurantFormat.service.js`), and the owner can change each afterwards.
Relations: `created_by`, `updated_by` -> `users` (FKs fk_restaurants_created_by / fk_restaurants_updated_by, added once `users` existed).
Rules: `code` UNIQUE; status and format CHECKs.
Added by: 1789636492039_create-restaurants-and-outlets; FKs in 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions; 1791950410000_food-formats (format); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_restaurants_live_session`).

#### `outlets`
Purpose: a branch of a restaurant. Business days, orders, KOTs, counters and stock can be per outlet.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `name`, `code` (varchar 30), `contact`, `address`, `status` (`active` / `inactive`), `own_store` (boolean, the outlet keeps its own stock store), `gst_number` (varchar 15, the outlet's own GSTIN), `order_prefix` / `invoice_prefix` (varchar 10, override the restaurant prefixes), `fssai_number` (varchar 14), `upi_vpa` / `upi_payee_name` (outlet UPI QR on bills).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants`; `created_by`, `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: UNIQUE (restaurant_id, code); chk_outlets_gst_number validates the 15-character GSTIN pattern; `fssai_number` must be exactly 14 digits.
Added by: 1789636492039_create-restaurants-and-outlets; 1789914000000_stock-counts-purchase-bills (own_store); 1789917000000_management-gst-filing (gst_number); 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog (prefixes); 1791340000002_bill-delivery-charge-fssai-upi-and-yearly-numbers (fssai, UPI).

### Users, roles and permissions

#### `users`
Purpose: every sign-in account. `restaurant_id` NULL means a platform account (Super Admin).
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `outlet_id`, `name`, `email`, `mobile`, `password_hash`, `status` (`active` / `inactive` / `locked`), `last_login_at`; session control `token_version` (bumped by password change / sign out everywhere, invalidates older tokens); `password_must_change` (boolean: someone else set the password, so the person chooses their own at their next sign-in, and until then the API answers 403 `password_change_required`); lockout `failed_login_count`, `first_failed_login_at`, `login_locked_until` (5 wrong passwords in 15 minutes locks for 15 minutes); personal till PIN `pin_hash` (bcrypt), `pin_set_at`, `pin_failed_count`, `pin_first_failed_at`, `pin_locked_until`; two-step sign-in `two_factor_secret`, `two_factor_enabled_at`, `two_factor_last_step` (last accepted TOTP step, blocks replay), `two_factor_pending`, `two_factor_pending_at` (secret being set up), `two_factor_recovery_salt` (varchar 32).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants`; `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `created_by`, `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: uq_users_email_lower (email unique case-insensitively, platform-wide); uq_users_restaurant_mobile (mobile unique per restaurant; platform accounts unique among themselves); chk_users_can_sign_in (email or mobile required); chk_users_pin_set (a PIN hash needs `pin_set_at`); chk_users_two_factor (`two_factor_secret` and `two_factor_enabled_at` are both set or both NULL); counters CHECK >= 0. Trigger `users_password_must_change` (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF `password_hash`, `password_must_change`; function `users_password_must_change()`) keeps the mark: every new login, and every change of `password_hash`, sets `password_must_change`, except the person's own change; an update that leaves the password as it is changes the mark in neither direction. Function `users_set_own_password(target, current_hash, new_hash)` is the person's own change (My profile, or the step at sign-in, after they typed their current password) and the one way to clear the mark: it names the person in the transaction's setting `app.own_password_of` for its own statement, replaces the password only while it is still `current_hash`, bumps `token_version` and returns the new version (NULL when the password changed meanwhile). Logins there before the rule were not marked.
Added by: 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions; 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions; 1790850000000_two-step-sign-in; 1790935000000_access-sign-in-tickets-and-pt-gender; 1791971000000_password-must-change (`password_must_change`, its trigger and `users_set_own_password`); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_users_live_session`).

#### `roles`
Purpose: named sets of permissions. `restaurant_id` NULL marks a system role shared by every restaurant; restaurants can add custom roles.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `code` (varchar 50), `name`, `description`, `is_system`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants`; `created_by`, `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (restaurant_id, code), so system role codes are unique too. System roles, created by migrations: SUPER_ADMIN, RESTAURANT_ADMIN, MANAGER, CASHIER, CAPTAIN, KITCHEN (1789636497042), DISPLAY "Collection display", a TV role that only sees the token collection screen (1790960000001), and ACCOUNTANT (reports, sales figures, expenses, supplier bills and payments, salaries, the activity log) and STORE_KEEPER (stock, counts, wastage, indents, purchase orders, receiving and returns, the supplier list) (1791590300000).
Added by: 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions; system roles from 1789636497042_seed-roles-and-permissions, 1790960000001_order-tokens-and-collection-screen and 1791590300000_accountant-and-store-keeper-roles.

#### `permissions`
Purpose: global catalogue of restaurant-module permission codes, defined by application code and created by migrations (the separate business module uses `business_permissions`).
Key columns: `code` (varchar 100), `module` (varchar 50, grouping), `name`, `description`.
Relations: referenced by `role_permissions.permission_id` and by `approvals.permission` (by code).
Rules: `code` UNIQUE. The codes are listed under [Restaurant permission codes](#restaurant-permission-codes).
Added by: 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions; rows from 1789636497042 onward.

#### `user_roles`
Purpose: which roles a user holds (many to many).
Key columns: `user_id`, `role_id`, `created_by`.
Relations: `user_id` -> `users` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `role_id` -> `roles` (ON DELETE CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (user_id, role_id).
Added by: 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions.

#### `role_permissions`
Purpose: which permissions a role grants (many to many).
Key columns: `role_id`, `permission_id`, `created_by`.
Relations: `role_id` -> `roles` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `permission_id` -> `permissions` (ON DELETE CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (role_id, permission_id). A migration that adds permission codes also grants them to the system roles that should hold them.
Added by: 1789636494559_create-users-roles-permissions; grants come with each permission migration.

#### `restaurant_access_settings`
Purpose: per-restaurant till access settings, owned by the access screens.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `idle_lock_minutes` (a shared till locks itself after this many idle minutes; 0 = never).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (ON DELETE CASCADE).
Rules: `idle_lock_minutes` between 0 and 240.
Added by: 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions.

### Manager approvals and sign-in security

#### `approvals`
Purpose: one-time manager approval tokens. A cashier lacking a permission asks, a manager enters their PIN, and the server issues a token for exactly that cashier, permission and (optionally) request; it lives about two minutes and is spent by the first successful action.
Key columns: `permission` (varchar 100, the permission code approved), `token_hash` (char 64, SHA-256 of the random token; the token itself is never stored), `requested_by`, `approved_by`, `target` (the one request it is for, e.g. `PATCH /bills/42/void`; NULL = any one request needing the permission), `label`, `note`, `scope` (jsonb facts the request is checked against, e.g. bill id and maximum amount), `expires_at`, `used_at`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `permission` -> `permissions(code)` (CASCADE); `requested_by`, `approved_by` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: `token_hash` UNIQUE; CHECK requested_by <> approved_by (nobody approves themselves).
Added by: 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions.

#### `user_sign_ins`
Purpose: history of every sign-in attempt against a known account (password, PIN switch, unlock, two-step code, recovery code), read by the owner per user.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `user_id`, `method` (`password` / `pin` / `unlock` / `two_factor` / `recovery_code`), `outcome` (`success` / `wrong_password` / `wrong_pin` / `locked` / `inactive` / `code_needed` / `setup_needed` / `wrong_code` / `two_factor_device`), `identifier` (what was typed: email, mobile or employee code), `ip_address`, `user_agent`, `trusted_device` (came from a remembered device), `two_factor` (`passed` / `failed` / `recovery_code` / `trusted_device` / `set_up`).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `user_id` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: method, outcome and two_factor CHECK lists. Attempts against unknown accounts are not stored. Insert-only (no updated_at).
Added by: 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions; 1790850000000_two-step-sign-in.

#### `user_trusted_devices`
Purpose: browsers that have signed in to an account with the right password. A remembered device keeps working through an account lockout with its own failure counter, so strangers' guesses cannot lock the owner out; it can also skip the two-step code for a while.
Key columns: `user_id`, `token_hash` (char 64, SHA-256 of the device token), `token_version` (the account's version when remembered; a bump forgets the device), `user_agent`, `failed_count`, `first_failed_at`, `locked_until`, `last_used_at`, `expires_at` (120 days after last use), `two_factor_passed_at`, `two_factor_trusted_until`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `user_id` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: `token_hash` UNIQUE; `failed_count` >= 0.
Added by: 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions; 1790850000000_two-step-sign-in (two-factor columns).

#### `user_recovery_codes`
Purpose: one-time recovery codes for two-step sign-in, stored as hashes.
Key columns: `user_id`, `code_hash` (char 64), `used_at`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `user_id` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: UNIQUE (user_id, code_hash); partial index idx_user_recovery_codes_unused on unused codes.
Added by: 1790850000000_two-step-sign-in.

#### `restaurant_two_factor_roles`
Purpose: which roles must use two-step sign-in in a restaurant.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `role_id`, `created_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `role_id` -> `roles` (CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (restaurant_id, role_id).
Added by: 1790850000000_two-step-sign-in.

#### `user_two_factor_spent_tickets`
Purpose: makes the short-lived sign-in ticket single-use (the ticket a right password earns while the two-step code is still to come). A used ticket id is recorded and refused if presented again; rows are only kept until the ticket would have expired.
Key columns: `ticket_id` (varchar 40, PK), `user_id`, `expires_at`, `spent_at`.
Relations: `user_id` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY on ticket_id (a ticket can be spent once); index on `expires_at` for cleanup.
Added by: 1790935000000_access-sign-in-tickets-and-pt-gender.

### Activity and notifications

#### `audit_logs`
Purpose: restaurant activity log ("who did what"), including the manager who approved an action.
Key columns: `user_id`, `user_name` (snapshot), `action` (varchar 50), `entity_type` (varchar 50), `entity_id`, `entity_label`, `details` (jsonb), `approved_by`, `approver_name`, `created_at`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `user_id`, `approved_by` -> `users`.
Rules: insert-only (no updated_at). Indexes by restaurant/time, entity, user and action.
Added by: 1789791748850_create-audit-log-and-gst-settings; 1789910000000_access-approvals-and-sessions (approver columns, user and action indexes).

#### `notification_reads`
Purpose: remembers which in-app alerts (computed live by providers) each person has already seen. When the underlying situation changes, the provider issues a new alert id, so it becomes unread again.
Key columns: `user_id`, `alert_id` (varchar 200), `seen_members` (text[], for summary alerts whose id stays the same: the members already seen), `read_at`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `user_id` -> `users` (CASCADE).
Rules: uq_notification_reads UNIQUE (restaurant_id, user_id, alert_id). No created_by: a person only marks their own alerts.
Added by: 1789904000000_create-notification-reads.

## Restaurant module

### Settings

#### `restaurant_settings`
Purpose: one row of configuration per restaurant (money, tax, numbering, printing, loyalty, cash drawer, GST identity).
Key columns: currency `currency_code` (INR), `currency_symbol`, `timezone` (Asia/Kolkata); tax `tax_mode` (`inclusive` / `exclusive`), `default_tax_percentage`, `service_charge_percentage`, `service_charge_order_types` (varchar[] subset of dine_in, takeaway, delivery, qr, online; default dine_in), `document_type` (`auto` / `tax_invoice` / `bill_of_supply`), `composition_percentage`, `composition_note`; identity `gst_number`, `fssai_number`, `gst_split` (show CGST/SGST split); numbering `order_prefix`, `invoice_prefix`, `invoice_numbering` (`continuous` / `financial_year`, the latter restarting every 1 April); printing `bill_header`, `bill_footer`, `print_paper_width` (`58mm` / `80mm` / `a4`), `print_copies` (1 to 5), `auto_print_bill`, `print_show_qr`, `receipt_logo_path`; stock `auto_consume_stock`; loyalty rules `loyalty_earn_amount`, `loyalty_point_value`, `loyalty_min_redeem`, `loyalty_earn_basis` (`total` / `pre_tax`), `loyalty_max_redeem_points`, `loyalty_max_redeem_percent`, `loyalty_expiry_days`; day and cash `day_cutoff_hour` (0 to 12), `cash_variance_threshold`, `cash_blind_count`; UPI `upi_vpa`, `upi_payee_name`.
Alerts and timings (Settings → Alerts and timings; each column's default, then its allowed range): theft control `alert_large_discount_percent` (20; 1 to 100), `alert_large_discount_amount` (1,000; 1 to 10,000,000), `alert_huge_discount_percent` (50; 1 to 100), `alert_reprint_count` (3; 2 to 20), `alert_cash_short_critical` (1,000; 1 to 10,000,000); deliveries `delivery_sla_minutes` (45; 10 to 240), `delivery_warn_minutes` (30; 5 to 235), `delivery_ready_wait_minutes` (10; 1 to 120), `delivery_out_warn_minutes` (45; 5 to 240), `delivery_out_critical_minutes` (60; 10 to 480); reservations `reservation_late_minutes` (15; 1 to 180), `reservation_last_seating_minutes` (30; 0 to 240), `reservation_slot_minutes` (30; one of 10, 15, 20, 30, 45, 60); time clock `timeclock_late_minutes` (15; 1 to 240), `timeclock_long_break_minutes` (45; 5 to 480), `timeclock_overtime_minutes` (15; 0 to 240), `timeclock_early_minutes` (60; 0 to 720); guests `guest_regular_visits` (3; 2 to 50), `guest_regular_days` (60; 7 to 730), `guest_lapsed_days` (45; 7 to 730), `guest_new_days` (30; 1 to 365), `guest_big_spender_percent` (10; 1 to 50); reorder plan `reorder_lookback_days` (14; 3 to 90), `reorder_safety_days` (2; 0 to 30), `reorder_horizon_days` (7; 1 to 30); `cash_denominations` (int[], the notes a cash count lists in rupees, `{500,200,100,50,20,10}`; 1 to 15 values, each 1 to 100,000). The kitchen's pass time is `kitchen_settings.pass_minutes`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` (PK) -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `created_by`, `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: percentages 0 to 100; loyalty values positive and bounded; chk_settings_service_charge_types; trigger trg_restaurant_settings_timezone (function check_settings_timezone) refuses a time zone name Postgres does not know or an Etc/posix/SystemV alias. Each alert and timing column has its range CHECK, plus chk_restaurant_settings_huge_discount (the huge discount % is at least the large one), chk_restaurant_settings_delivery_warn (the amber mark comes before the delivery promise) and chk_restaurant_settings_delivery_out (the critical run time is after its warning).
Loyalty on or off is not a column here: it is the Loyalty points feature (`guest_loyalty`, see [Restaurant features](#restaurant-features)), and `restaurantRepository.findSettings` returns a computed `loyalty_enabled` read from `restaurant_features()`.
Added by: 1789723751286_create-restaurant-settings-and-hours; 1789791748850_create-audit-log-and-gst-settings; 1789817853331_create-loyalty; 1789854000000_add-print-settings; 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls; 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog; 1790810000000_cash-drawers-and-loyalty-rules; 1791340000002_bill-delivery-charge-fssai-upi-and-yearly-numbers; 1791640001000_restaurant-rule-settings (alerts and timings).

#### `business_hours`
Purpose: opening hours per weekday, restaurant-wide (`outlet_id` NULL) or per outlet; supports several service periods a day.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `day_of_week` (0 to 6), `is_open`, `open_time`, `close_time`, `periods` (jsonb array of `{openTime, closeTime}`).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets` (CASCADE).
Rules: UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (restaurant_id, outlet_id, day_of_week); chk_business_hours_times (an open day needs both times); chk_business_hours_periods_array (`periods` must be a JSON array).
Added by: 1789723751286_create-restaurant-settings-and-hours; 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog (periods).

#### `floor_settings`
Purpose: floor-plan settings per restaurant.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `cleaning_minutes` (how long a table stays in cleaning after settlement, 0 to 120, default 10).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Added by: 1789921000000_floor-plan-turns.

#### `service_settings`
Purpose: the service desk's settings: daily token numbers, the guest ordering web address, and the kinds of order the POS takes.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `tokens_enabled` (default off), `token_order_types` (text[] subset of takeaway, delivery, online; default takeaway), `collection_clear_minutes` (1 to 120; a ready token leaves the collection screen after this long), `guest_web_address` (varchar 200), `pos_order_types` (text[], the kinds of order the POS offers: a non-empty subset of dine_in, takeaway, delivery; default all three, a cloud kitchen takes no dine-in), `default_order_type` (the kind a new order starts on; default dine_in, a café starts on takeaway).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: service_settings_pos_types (non-empty, only those three kinds); service_settings_default_type (the default is one of the kinds offered).
Added by: 1790960000001_order-tokens-and-collection-screen; 1790960000002_guest-ordering-web-address; 1791950410000_food-formats (POS order types).

#### `document_counters`
Purpose: last number handed out per document series, so concurrent tills get distinct numbers (the row is locked until the transaction commits).
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `series` (varchar 40, e.g. `BILL`, `BILL:<prefix>`, `PO`, `PR`), `last_number`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (restaurant_id, series); `last_number` >= 0.
Added by: 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls; 1789914000000_stock-counts-purchase-bills (PO and PR series); 1790800000000_billing-series-and-guest-accounts (series up to 40 characters, per-prefix bill series).

#### `till_discount_limits`
Purpose: maximum on-the-spot till discount a role may give on its own, per restaurant. A role with no row has no cap; going over needs someone whose cap covers it.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `role_id`, `max_percent` (0 to 100).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `role_id` -> `roles` (CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (restaurant_id, role_id).
Added by: 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls.

### Menu

#### `categories`
Purpose: menu categories.
Key columns: `name`, `description`, `sort_order`, `status`, `brand_id` (the brand that sells it; NULL = a category every brand sells, such as drinks or desserts).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `brand_id` -> `restaurant_brands` (RESTRICT).
Rules: uq_categories_restaurant_name (name unique per restaurant, case-insensitive). Index idx_categories_brand (restaurant_id, brand_id).
Added by: 1789723754642_create-menu-tables; 1791950411000_kitchen-brands (brand_id).

#### `restaurant_brands`
Purpose: the names one kitchen sells under (a cloud kitchen that sells its rice bowls under one name and its wraps under another, each its own listing on Swiggy and Zomato). A category belongs to one brand or to all; an order is taken under a brand, which its KOT and bill print and the brand report counts. The kitchen, stock, staff, GST and the business day stay one.
Key columns: `name` (varchar 80, not blank), `tagline` (varchar 150), `color` (varchar 20), `sort_order`, `status` (`active` / `inactive`), `created_by`, `updated_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); referenced by `categories.brand_id` and `orders.brand_id` (both ON DELETE RESTRICT, so a brand in use is switched off, not deleted).
Rules: uq_restaurant_brands_name (name unique per restaurant, case-insensitive); status CHECK. The brand endpoints need the `brands` feature (off by default; the cloud kitchen format switches it on).
Added by: 1791950411000_kitchen-brands.

#### `products`
Purpose: menu dishes (also combos when `is_combo`).
Key columns: `category_id`, `name`, `code`, `description`, `price`, `tax_percentage`, `tax_rate_id`, `tax_mode` (`default` / `inclusive` / `exclusive`, overrides the restaurant mode), `mrp`, `food_type` (`veg` / `non_veg` / `egg`), `is_available`, `sort_order`, `status`, `is_combo`, `image_path`, `addon_groups` (jsonb array of add-on choice groups), `availability_schedule` (jsonb), `combo_choice_groups` (jsonb), `kitchen_name` (varchar 150, name printed for the cook in any script), `sold_by` (`unit` / `weight`, default unit; a dish or bake sold by weight is priced per kg and its order quantity is the weight in kg, 0.25 = 250 g).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `category_id` -> `categories`; `tax_rate_id` -> `tax_rates`.
Rules: uq_products_restaurant_name (case-insensitive name per restaurant); uq_products_restaurant_code (upper-cased code per restaurant when set); price >= 0; `addon_groups` must be a JSON array; `sold_by` CHECK; deferred constraint trigger trg_products_size_prices_not_negative (function menu_size_price_not_negative) refuses a base price change that would make any active size cost below zero.
Added by: 1789723754642_create-menu-tables; 1789830000000_create-tax-rates (tax_rate_id); 1789836000000_create-combos (is_combo); 1789848000000_add-product-images; 1789915000000_menu-data-safety (trigger); 1790726600000_addon-choice-groups; 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog (tax_mode, mrp); 1790727000000_service-readiness (availability_schedule, combo_choice_groups); 1791330002000_kitchen-names; 1791950410000_food-formats (sold_by).

#### `product_variants`
Purpose: sizes of a dish (half / full), priced as a delta on the dish price.
Key columns: `product_id`, `name`, `price_delta`, `is_default`, `sort_order`, `status`, `recipe_factor` (0 to 10, scales the dish recipe for this size), `kitchen_name` (varchar 100).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `product_id` -> `products` (CASCADE).
Rules: uq_product_variants_name (case-insensitive per dish); deferred constraint trigger trg_product_variants_price_not_negative (menu_size_price_not_negative) refuses an active size whose final price (dish price + delta) is below zero.
Added by: 1789731796243_create-menu-variants-and-modifiers; 1789914000000_stock-counts-purchase-bills (recipe_factor); 1789915000000_menu-data-safety; 1791330002000_kitchen-names.

#### `modifiers`
Purpose: priced add-ons (extra cheese).
Key columns: `name`, `price`, `status`, `kitchen_name` (varchar 100).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Rules: uq_modifiers_restaurant_name (case-insensitive per restaurant); price >= 0.
Added by: 1789731796243_create-menu-variants-and-modifiers; 1791330002000_kitchen-names.

#### `product_modifiers`
Purpose: which add-ons are offered on which dish.
Key columns: `product_id`, `modifier_id`.
Relations: `product_id` -> `products` (CASCADE); `modifier_id` -> `modifiers` (CASCADE).
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (product_id, modifier_id).
Added by: 1789731796243_create-menu-variants-and-modifiers.

#### `product_recipes`
Purpose: ingredients consumed per dish (optionally per size) for stock deduction and food cost.
Key columns: `product_id`, `product_variant_id` (NULL = whole dish), `inventory_item_id`, `quantity` (numeric 14,4, > 0).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `product_id` -> `products` (CASCADE); `product_variant_id` -> `product_variants` (CASCADE); `inventory_item_id` -> `inventory_items`.
Rules: UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (product_id, product_variant_id, inventory_item_id).
Added by: 1789791746176_create-recipes.

#### `modifier_recipes`
Purpose: ingredients consumed by an add-on.
Key columns: `modifier_id`, `inventory_item_id`, `quantity` (> 0).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `modifier_id` -> `modifiers` (CASCADE); `inventory_item_id` -> `inventory_items`.
Rules: UNIQUE (modifier_id, inventory_item_id).
Added by: 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog.

#### `tax_rates`
Purpose: named GST rates with HSN/SAC code and cess, assigned to dishes.
Key columns: `name`, `percentage`, `hsn_code`, `cess_percentage`, `is_default`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); referenced by `products.tax_rate_id`.
Rules: uq_tax_rates_restaurant_name (case-insensitive); uq_tax_rates_default (one default rate per restaurant, partial unique index); percentages 0 to 100. A new restaurant starts with Exempt, GST 5% (restaurant, the default, SAC 996331), GST 18% and GST 40% (`createRestaurant` in `restaurant.repository.js`). Trigger `tax_rates_in_change` (function `business_tax_rates_in_change`): a rate a scheduled GST rate change moves dishes to cannot be switched off before their date ([GST rates by date](#gst-rates-by-date)); a rate a change has named is never deleted.
Added by: 1789830000000_create-tax-rates; 1791973000000 (trigger `tax_rates_in_change`).

#### `combo_items`
Purpose: the dishes (and sizes) inside a combo dish.
Key columns: `combo_product_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `quantity`, `sort_order`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `combo_product_id` -> `products` (CASCADE); `product_id` -> `products`; `product_variant_id` -> `product_variants`.
Rules: uq_combo_items UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (combo_product_id, product_id, product_variant_id); chk_combo_not_self; deferred constraint trigger trg_combo_items_one_level (function menu_combo_one_level) refuses a combo inside a combo.
Added by: 1789836000000_create-combos; 1789915000000_menu-data-safety.

#### `price_windows`
Purpose: time-based pricing windows (happy hour), restaurant-wide or per outlet.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `name`, `days_of_week` (smallint[], 1 to 7 entries), `start_time`, `end_time`, `valid_from`, `valid_to`, `priority`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets` (CASCADE).
Rules: uq_price_windows_restaurant_name (case-insensitive); chk_price_window_dates (valid_to >= valid_from).
Added by: 1789842000000_create-price-windows.

#### `price_window_items`
Purpose: the price of a dish (or size) during a price window.
Key columns: `price_window_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `price`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `price_window_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id` all ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: uq_price_window_items UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (price_window_id, product_id, product_variant_id); price >= 0.
Added by: 1789842000000_create-price-windows.

#### `product_selling_rules`
Purpose: context-specific price, availability and packaging charge for a dish by outlet, sales channel and/or order type.
Key columns: `product_id`, `outlet_id`, `channel_id`, `order_type` (`dine_in` / `takeaway` / `delivery` / `qr` / `online`), `price` (NULL = normal price), `is_available`, `packaging_charge` (>= 0).
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `product_id`, `outlet_id` -> `outlets`, `channel_id` -> `sales_channels`, all ON DELETE CASCADE; `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: CHECK at least one of outlet, channel or order type is set; UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (product_id, outlet_id, channel_id, order_type).
Added by: 1790726400000_contextual-menu-prices; 1790726800000_context-packaging-charge.

#### `menu_sold_out`
Purpose: "86" marks: a dish or size sold out for one business day, with who restored it.
Key columns: `business_day_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `note`, `restored_at`, `restored_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `business_day_id` -> `business_days`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id` all ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: uq_menu_sold_out_live (one live mark per day, dish and size while `restored_at` IS NULL).
Added by: 1789912000000_pos-floor-moves-and-sold-out; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_menu_sold_out_live`).

### Tables and floor

#### `floors`
Purpose: floors of the dining room.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `name`, `sort_order`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`.
Rules: uq_floors_restaurant_name (case-insensitive per restaurant).
Added by: 1789731793649_create-table-management.

#### `dining_areas`
Purpose: sections within a floor (AC hall, terrace).
Key columns: `floor_id`, `name`, `sort_order`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `floor_id` -> `floors`.
Rules: UNIQUE (floor_id, name).
Added by: 1789731793649_create-table-management.

#### `dining_tables`
Purpose: physical tables with their QR token and live status.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `floor_id`, `area_id`, `table_no`, `capacity` (1 to 100), `qr_token` (varchar 64, guest QR ordering), `table_status` (`available` / `occupied` / `reserved` / `cleaning`), `status`, `ready_at`, `ready_by` (when the table was last marked ready after cleaning).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `floor_id` -> `floors`; `area_id` -> `dining_areas`; `ready_by` -> `users` (SET NULL).
Rules: `qr_token` UNIQUE; UNIQUE (restaurant_id, table_no).
Added by: 1789731793649_create-table-management; 1789921000000_floor-plan-turns (ready_at, ready_by); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_dining_tables_live`).

#### `captain_assignments`
Purpose: which captain has which floor, section or table on a business date ("My tables", call routing). The most specific assignment wins.
Key columns: `shift_date`, `user_id`, `floor_id`, `area_id`, `table_id`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `user_id`, `floor_id`, `area_id` -> `dining_areas`, `table_id` -> `dining_tables`, all ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: CHECK exactly one of floor_id, area_id, table_id is set; uq_captain_floor / uq_captain_area / uq_captain_table (one captain per floor, area or table per restaurant and date). The rows are read only while the restaurant's `captain_sections` part is switched on: while it is off no table has a captain, and the day's rows stay as they were.
Added by: 1791330000003_captain-sections.

### Orders and kitchen

#### `orders`
Purpose: a guest order (dine-in, takeaway, delivery, QR or online) from opening to billing.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `business_day_id`, `order_number`, `order_type` (`dine_in` / `takeaway` / `delivery` / `qr` / `online`), `source` (`pos` / `captain` / `qr` / `website` / `phone` / `partner`), `table_id`, `customer_id`, `customer_name`, `customer_mobile`, `guest_count`, `order_status` (`open` / `billed` / `completed` / `cancelled`), `kitchen_status` (`pending` / `preparing` / `ready` / `served`), `subtotal`, `tax_amount`, `total_amount`, `notes`, `cancel_reason`, `placed_at`, `completed_at`, `merged_into_order_id`; delivery `channel_id`, `channel_commission_percent` (0 to 50, snapshot), `delivery_status` (`pending` / `preparing` / `ready` / `out_for_delivery` / `delivered` / `cancelled`), `delivery_address`, `delivery_landmark`, `rider_employee_id`, `partner_rider_name`, `external_order_ref`, `delivery_eta_minutes` (5 to 240), `delivery_preparing_at`, `delivery_ready_at`, `dispatched_at`, `delivered_at`, `delivery_cancelled_at`, `delivery_cancel_reason`, `delivery_charge`, `delivery_charge_tax_percent`, `delivery_charge_waived_at`, `delivery_charge_waived_by`, `delivery_charge_waived_reason`; tokens `token_no`, `token_collected_at`, `token_collected_by`; brand `brand_id` (the brand the order was taken under, printed on its KOTs and bill; NULL = the kitchen's own name).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `business_day_id` -> `business_days`; `table_id` -> `dining_tables`; `customer_id` -> `customers`; `merged_into_order_id` -> `orders` (SET NULL); `channel_id` -> `sales_channels` (SET NULL); `rider_employee_id` -> `employees` (SET NULL); `delivery_charge_waived_by`, `token_collected_by` -> `users`; `brand_id` -> `restaurant_brands` (RESTRICT).
Rules: UNIQUE (restaurant_id, order_number); chk_orders_table (dine-in needs a table); uq_orders_open_table (one open order per table); uq_orders_day_token (a token number is never reused within a business day); chk_orders_delivery_out (out for delivery needs `dispatched_at` and a rider or partner rider name); chk_orders_delivery_cancel_reason; chk_orders_delivery_waiver (a waived charge needs a reason); amounts >= 0. Triggers: trg_orders_free_joined_tables (function free_joined_tables) frees joined tables when the order leaves open/billed; trg_orders_erased_guest (keep_erased_guests_unlinked) refuses to link an erased guest by nulling `customer_id`. Partial indexes for tables in use, settled tables, deliveries, riders, channels, customers and waiting tokens support the POS screens; idx_orders_brand (restaurant_id, brand_id) where a brand is set serves the brand report.
Added by: 1789790365080_create-orders; 1789905000000_create-deliveries-and-channels; 1789908000000_add-guest-insights (index); 1789911000000_orders-core-edits-and-cancellations; 1789912000000_pos-floor-moves-and-sold-out; 1789916000000_crm-guest-book-privacy; 1789921000000_floor-plan-turns (index); 1790820000002 (the open-table index); 1790960000001_order-tokens-and-collection-screen; 1791330000000_delivery-charge-on-orders; 1791950411000_kitchen-brands (brand_id); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_orders_live`).

#### `order_items`
Purpose: dish lines on an order, with name, price and tax snapshots.
Key columns: `order_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `item_name`, `variant_name`, `quantity`, `unit_price`, `modifier_total`, `tax_percentage`, `cess_percentage`, `hsn_code`, `price_includes_tax`, `mrp`, `line_subtotal`, `line_tax`, `line_total`, `item_status` (`pending` / `preparing` / `ready` / `served` / `cancelled`), `notes`, `price_window_id`, `course_no` (1 to 20), `course_name`, `fire_status` (`held` / `fired`), `fired_at`, `fired_by`, `combo_parts` (jsonb), `created_by`.
Relations: `order_id` -> `orders` (CASCADE); `product_id` -> `products`; `product_variant_id` -> `product_variants`; `price_window_id` -> `price_windows`; `fired_by`, `created_by` -> `users`.
Rules: quantity > 0, amounts >= 0. Triggers on insert: trg_order_items_cess (stamp_order_item_cess) copies the cess % from the dish's tax rate; zz_order_item_composition (clear_composition_order_cess) zeroes cess when the restaurant issues bills of supply (composition scheme).
Added by: 1789790365080_create-orders; 1789830000000_create-tax-rates (hsn_code); 1789842000000_create-price-windows; 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls (cess); 1789915000000_menu-data-safety (indexes); 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog (tax mode, mrp, courses); 1790727000000_service-readiness (combo_parts, composition trigger); 1790820000000_kitchen-round-sender (fired_by); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_order_items_live`).

#### `order_item_modifiers`
Purpose: add-ons chosen on an order line, with name and price snapshots.
Key columns: `order_item_id`, `modifier_id`, `modifier_name`, `price`.
Relations: `order_item_id` -> `order_items` (CASCADE); `modifier_id` -> `modifiers`.
Added by: 1789790365080_create-orders; 1789915000000_menu-data-safety (index).

#### `order_counters`
Purpose: last order number per restaurant, locked per order so concurrent tills never collide.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `last_number`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Added by: 1789911000000_orders-core-edits-and-cancellations.

#### `outlet_order_counters`
Purpose: last order number per outlet (or restaurant-wide when `outlet_id` is NULL), for outlet-prefixed order numbers.
Key columns: `restaurant_id`, `outlet_id`, `last_number`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `outlet_id` ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: uq_outlet_order_counters UNIQUE (restaurant_id, COALESCE(outlet_id, 0)).
Added by: 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog.

#### `order_token_counters`
Purpose: last pickup token number handed out per business day (tokens restart at 1 each day).
Key columns: `business_day_id` (PK), `restaurant_id`, `last_number`.
Relations: `business_day_id` -> `business_days` (CASCADE); `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Added by: 1790960000001_order-tokens-and-collection-screen.

#### `order_item_cancellations`
Purpose: log of every reduced or removed dish, before and after the KOT, for the void report.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `business_day_id`, `order_id`, `order_item_id`, `product_id`, `item_name`, `variant_name`, `modifiers_label`, `quantity`, `unit_price`, `amount`, `after_kot`, `kitchen_stage` (`new` / `cooking` / `ready` / `served`), `reason_code` (`guest_changed_mind` / `wrong_order` / `delay` / `quality` / `other`), `reason_note`, `source` (`edit` / `item` / `order`), `cancelled_by`, `approved_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `order_id` -> `orders` (CASCADE); `outlet_id`, `business_day_id`, `order_item_id`, `product_id`, `cancelled_by`, `approved_by` ON DELETE SET NULL.
Rules: chk_cancellation_reason (after the KOT a reason is required); chk_cancellation_other_note ("other" needs a note). Insert-only.
Added by: 1789911000000_orders-core-edits-and-cancellations.

#### `order_joined_tables`
Purpose: extra tables joined to an order (a party spread over several tables).
Key columns: `order_id`, `table_id`, `released_at`, `released_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `order_id`, `table_id` ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: uq_order_joined_tables_live (a table is joined to an order once while not released). Freed by trigger trg_orders_free_joined_tables on `orders`.
Added by: 1789912000000_pos-floor-moves-and-sold-out; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_order_joined_tables_live`).

#### `order_moves`
Purpose: history of table moves, joins, unjoins and dish transfers between orders.
Key columns: `business_day_id`, `move_type` (`table` / `join` / `dishes` / `unjoin`), `from_order_id`, `to_order_id`, `from_table_id`, `to_table_id`, `from_table_no`, `to_table_no`, `items` (jsonb), `amount`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `from_order_id`, `to_order_id` ON DELETE CASCADE; `business_day_id`, table ids ON DELETE SET NULL.
Added by: 1789912000000_pos-floor-moves-and-sold-out.

#### `restaurant_preorders`
Purpose: an order for a later day (a cake, a party tray) with the message, an advance and the time it is due. It moves booked → in_production → ready → collected (or cancelled); on collection it becomes a takeaway order on the open business day, and the advance is put on that order, so its bill takes the advance first.
Key columns: `outlet_id` (where it was booked; NULL = the main counter), `preorder_number` (varchar 20, PRE-0001…), `customer_id`, `due_at`, `fulfilment` (`pickup` / `delivery`), `delivery_address`, `notes` (varchar 500), `status` (`booked` / `in_production` / `ready` / `collected` / `cancelled`), `estimated_total` (>= 0), `advance_id` (an ordinary guest advance), `order_id` (the order it became), `cancel_reason`, `collected_at`, `created_by`, `updated_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets` (SET NULL); `customer_id` -> `customers` (RESTRICT); `advance_id` -> `customer_advances` (SET NULL); `order_id` -> `orders` (SET NULL).
Rules: UNIQUE (restaurant_id, preorder_number); cancelled exactly when there is a `cancel_reason`; a collected pre-order has its order; delivery needs an address (CHECKs). Partial index restaurant_preorders_due (restaurant_id, due_at) on booked, in-production and ready rows. The endpoints need the `preorders` feature (off by default; the café and bakery formats switch it on), and taking an advance also needs `advances`.
Added by: 1791950410000_food-formats; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_restaurant_preorders_live`).

#### `restaurant_preorder_items`
Purpose: the dishes on a pre-order, with what the baker reads.
Key columns: `preorder_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `quantity` (numeric 10,2, > 0), `unit_price` (>= 0), `notes` (varchar 300: the message on the cake, the flavour, the shape).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `preorder_id` -> `restaurant_preorders` (CASCADE); `product_id` -> `products` (RESTRICT); `product_variant_id` -> `product_variants` (RESTRICT).
Rules: index restaurant_preorder_items_order (restaurant_id, preorder_id).
Added by: 1791950410000_food-formats.

#### `kitchen_stations`
Purpose: kitchen sections (tandoor, bar) that receive KOTs. A new restaurant starts with one, Main kitchen, as its default.
Key columns: `name`, `color` (orange, red, amber, green, teal, blue, violet, pink, slate), `sort_order`, `is_default`, `status`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Rules: uq_kitchen_stations_name (case-insensitive); uq_kitchen_stations_default (one default station per restaurant); chk_kitchen_stations_default_active (the default must be active).
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display.

#### `kitchen_category_stations`
Purpose: routes a menu category to a station.
Key columns: `category_id` (PK), `station_id`.
Relations: `category_id` -> `categories`, `restaurant_id`, `station_id` -> `kitchen_stations`, all CASCADE.
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display.

#### `kitchen_product_stations`
Purpose: routes one dish to a station, overriding its category.
Key columns: `product_id` (PK), `station_id`.
Relations: `product_id` -> `products`, `restaurant_id`, `station_id` -> `kitchen_stations`, all CASCADE.
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display.

#### `kitchen_settings`
Purpose: kitchen display settings per restaurant.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `warn_minutes` (1 to 120), `late_minutes` (2 to 180), `live_since`, `kitchen_name_mode` (`menu` / `kitchen` / `both`, which dish name KOTs and the screen show), `pass_minutes` (1 to 60, default 8: food plated and waiting at the pass this long is going cold).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE).
Rules: chk_kitchen_settings_order (late > warn); chk_kitchen_settings_name_mode.
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display; 1791330002000_kitchen-names; 1791640001000_restaurant-rule-settings (pass_minutes).

#### `kots`
Purpose: kitchen order tickets, one per order round per station.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `business_day_id`, `order_id`, `station_id`, `kot_number`, `kot_status` (`new` / `cooking` / `ready` / `served` / `cancelled`), `created_at`, `started_at`, `ready_at`, `served_at`, `cancelled_at`, `recalled_at`, `recall_count`, `cleared_reason` (`day_closed` / `order_closed` / `manager`), `print_count`, `last_printed_at`, `started_by`, `ready_by`, `served_by`, `sent_by` (who fired the round), `moved_from_table_no`, `moved_at`, `split_from_kot_id`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `business_day_id` -> `business_days`; `order_id` -> `orders` (CASCADE); `station_id` -> `kitchen_stations`; `split_from_kot_id` -> `kots` (SET NULL); user columns -> `users`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business_day_id, kot_number); chk_kots_cleared_served (a cleared ticket must be served). Partial indexes for open and served tickets.
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display; 1789912000000_pos-floor-moves-and-sold-out; 1790820000000_kitchen-round-sender; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_kots_live`).

#### `kot_counters`
Purpose: last KOT number per business day.
Key columns: `business_day_id` (PK), `restaurant_id`, `last_number`.
Relations: `business_day_id` -> `business_days` (CASCADE); `restaurant_id` (CASCADE).
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display.

#### `kot_items`
Purpose: lines on a KOT, with name snapshots as cut.
Key columns: `kot_id`, `order_id`, `order_item_id`, `product_id`, `line_key`, `item_name`, `variant_name`, `modifiers` (jsonb), `components` (jsonb, combo parts), `notes`, `food_type`, `quantity`, `cancelled_quantity`, `is_done`, `done_at`, `done_by`, `kitchen_name`, `variant_kitchen_name`, `modifier_kitchen_names` (jsonb, parallel to modifiers).
Relations: `kot_id` -> `kots` (CASCADE); `restaurant_id`, `order_id` (CASCADE); `order_item_id`, `product_id` ON DELETE SET NULL; `done_by` -> `users`.
Rules: quantity > 0; 0 <= cancelled_quantity <= quantity.
Added by: 1789901000000_create-kitchen-display; 1791330002000_kitchen-names; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_kot_items_live`).

#### `kot_change_slips`
Purpose: printed slips telling a station about a cancellation, a cancelled order or a table change after its KOT went out.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `business_day_id`, `order_id`, `station_id`, `kind` (`cancel` / `order_cancelled` / `table_changed`), `order_number`, `order_type`, `table_no`, `from_table_no`, `to_table_no`, `kot_ids` (bigint[]), `kot_numbers` (int[]), `lines` (jsonb), `reason`, `changed_by`, `approved_by`, `print_count`, `first_printed_at`, `last_printed_at`, `last_printed_by`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `order_id`, `station_id` ON DELETE CASCADE; `outlet_id`, `business_day_id` ON DELETE SET NULL.
Rules: partial index idx_kot_change_slips_unprinted on `print_count = 0`.
Added by: 1790960000000_kitchen-change-slips; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_kot_change_slips_live`).

#### `kot_time_repairs`
Purpose: a backup kept by migration 1790820000001: the earlier `created_at` of each KOT it corrected (a ticket whose start, ready or served time came before its creation).
Key columns: `kot_id` (PK), `old_created_at`, `repaired_at`.
Relations: `kot_id` -> `kots` (CASCADE).
Added by: 1790820000001_repair-kitchen-ticket-times.

#### `table_requests`
Purpose: guest requests from a table QR (order, call waiter, bill, water, other) and website takeaway/delivery orders.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `table_id` (NULL for website orders), `business_day_id`, `request_type` (`order` / `call_waiter` / `bill` / `water` / `other`), `status` (`pending` / `processing` / `accepted` / `rejected` / `acknowledged` / `done` / `cancelled`), `client_key` (idempotency key), `guest_name`, `guest_mobile`, `notes`, `item_count`, `estimated_total`, `order_id`, `order_action` (`created` / `added`), `kitchen_reached` (`ready` / `served`), `reject_reason`, `repeat_count`, `last_asked_at`, `acknowledged_at/by`, `resolved_at/by`; online `channel` (`qr` / `website`), `online_link_id`, `service_type` (`takeaway` / `delivery`), `delivery_address`, `delivery_landmark`, `payment_mode` (`pay_at_pickup` / `cash_on_delivery`), `delivery_charge`, `guest_token`; `privacy_consents` (TEXT[]: what the guest ticked on the ordering page, `bills_digital` and `offers`, recorded as their consents when the order is accepted) and `privacy_notice_id` (the notice the page showed).
Relations: `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `table_id` -> `dining_tables` (CASCADE); `business_day_id`, `order_id`, `online_link_id` -> `online_ordering_links` ON DELETE SET NULL.
Rules: chk_table_requests_flow (order requests use pending/processing/accepted/rejected/cancelled; others use pending/acknowledged/done/cancelled); chk_table_requests_reject_reason; chk_table_requests_client_key (8 to 64 of A-Z, a-z, 0-9, hyphen); chk_table_requests_channel (a QR request has a table and no service type; a website request has no table, is an order, and has service type, payment mode, guest token, name and mobile, plus an address for delivery); uq_table_requests_client_key (per table); uq_table_requests_open_call (one open call_waiter / bill / water per table); uq_table_requests_guest_token; uq_table_requests_online_key (per online link); `table_requests_privacy_consents` (only those two, with the notice when any); `privacy_notice_id` -> `business_privacy_notices`.
Added by: 1789903000000_create-table-requests; 1791330003000_online-ordering; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_table_requests_live`); 1791977000000_personal-data (`privacy_consents`, `privacy_notice_id`).

#### `table_request_items`
Purpose: dishes in a guest order request, priced as the guest saw them.
Key columns: `request_id`, `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `modifier_ids` (bigint[]), `item_name`, `variant_name`, `modifiers` (jsonb), `food_type`, `quantity` (1 to 20), `notes`, `unit_price`, `line_total`, `sort_order`, `order_item_id` (the order line it became), `combo_selections` (jsonb).
Relations: `request_id` -> `table_requests` (CASCADE); `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `product_id`, `product_variant_id`, `order_item_id` ON DELETE SET NULL.
Added by: 1789903000000_create-table-requests; 1790727000000_service-readiness (combo_selections).

#### `table_request_settings`
Purpose: per-restaurant switches for QR table ordering and calls.
Key columns: `restaurant_id` (PK), `ordering_enabled`, `calls_enabled`, `guest_note`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `updated_by` -> `users`.
Added by: 1789903000000_create-table-requests.

### Reservations and online

#### `reservations`
Purpose: table bookings, taken by staff or requested online.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `reference_no`, `customer_id`, `guest_name`, `guest_mobile`, `party_size` (1 to 100), `reservation_date`, `start_time`, `duration_minutes` (15 to 480), `starts_at` / `ends_at` (generated, stored), `table_id`, `capacity_override`, `capacity_override_reason`, `occasion` (`birthday` / `anniversary` / `business` / `date_night` / `other`), `special_requests`, `source` (`phone` / `walk_in` / `online` / `app` / `other`), `internal_notes`, `status` (`requested` / `booked` / `confirmed` / `seated` / `completed` / `cancelled` / `no_show` / `declined`), `order_id`, `cancel_reason`, `guest_token` (guest's link to view or cancel an online request), `confirmed_at`, `seated_at`, `completed_at`, `cancelled_at`, `no_show_at`, `declined_at`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `customer_id` -> `customers`, `table_id` -> `dining_tables`, `order_id` -> `orders`, all ON DELETE SET NULL. Extra tables in `reservation_tables`.
Rules: UNIQUE (restaurant_id, reference_no); chk_reservations_cancel_reason; chk_reservations_decline_reason (declined needs `cancel_reason`); chk_reservations_seated_table (seated needs a table); chk_reservations_request_no_table (an online request has no table yet); uq_reservations_guest_token; trigger trg_reservations_erased_guest (keep_erased_guests_unlinked) unlinks erased guests.
Added by: 1789902000000_create-reservations; 1789916000000_crm-guest-book-privacy (trigger); 1790960000003_bookings-across-tables (capacity_override_reason); 1791330001000_online-table-booking (requested/declined, guest_token); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_reservations_live`).

#### `reservation_tables`
Purpose: all tables held by a booking that spans several tables.
Key columns: `reservation_id`, `table_id`.
Relations: `reservation_id`, `table_id`, `restaurant_id` ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: PRIMARY KEY (reservation_id, table_id).
Added by: 1790960000003_bookings-across-tables; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_reservation_tables_live`).

#### `waitlist_entries`
Purpose: walk-in waitlist.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `customer_id`, `guest_name`, `guest_mobile`, `party_size`, `quoted_minutes` (0 to 240), `notes`, `status` (`waiting` / `seated` / `left` / `removed`), `joined_at`, `notified_at`, `resolved_at`, `table_id`, `order_id`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` (CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `customer_id`, `table_id`, `order_id` ON DELETE SET NULL.
Rules: chk_waitlist_resolved (anything but waiting needs `resolved_at`); trigger trg_waitlist_entries_erased_guest unlinks erased guests.
Added by: 1789902000000_create-reservations; 1789916000000_crm-guest-book-privacy; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_waitlist_entries_live`).

#### `online_booking_settings`
Purpose: the public online table-booking page per restaurant or outlet and its limits.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `token` (varchar 64, public link), `is_enabled`, `max_party_size` (1 to 50), `seat_share_percent` (10 to 100, share of seats bookable online), `min_notice_minutes` (0 to 1440), `days_ahead` (1 to 180), `duration_minutes` (30 to 360), `max_open_per_mobile` (1 to 10), `blocked_dates` (date[]), `guest_note`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `outlet_id` ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: `token` UNIQUE; UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (restaurant_id, outlet_id).
Added by: 1791330001000_online-table-booking.

#### `online_ordering_links`
Purpose: the public online ordering (takeaway / delivery) link per restaurant or outlet and its rules.
Key columns: `outlet_id`, `token` (varchar 64), `is_enabled`, `takeaway_enabled`, `delivery_enabled`, `min_delivery_order`, `pickup_minutes` (5 to 180), `delivery_minutes` (10 to 240), `max_open_per_mobile` (1 to 10), `delivery_area`, `guest_note`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `outlet_id` ON DELETE CASCADE; referenced by `table_requests.online_link_id`.
Rules: `token` UNIQUE; UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (restaurant_id, outlet_id); chk_online_links_service (takeaway or delivery must be on).
Added by: 1791330003000_online-ordering.

#### `sales_channels`
Purpose: where delivery orders come from (own delivery, aggregators such as Swiggy and Zomato, phone, website), with commission and delivery charge rules.
Key columns: `name`, `channel_type` (`own` / `aggregator` / `phone` / `website`), `commission_percent` (0 to 50), `color`, `sort_order`, `status`, `delivery_charge`, `delivery_free_above`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); referenced by `orders.channel_id` and `product_selling_rules.channel_id`.
Rules: uq_sales_channels_name (case-insensitive per restaurant); `delivery_free_above` > 0 when set. A new restaurant starts with Own delivery, Swiggy (22%), Zomato (25%), Phone and Website.
Added by: 1789905000000_create-deliveries-and-channels; 1791330000000_delivery-charge-on-orders.

#### `customer_addresses`
Purpose: a guest's saved delivery addresses, most recently used first.
Key columns: `customer_id`, `label` (default Home), `address` (non-blank), `landmark`, `last_used_at`, `use_count`.
Relations: `restaurant_id`, `customer_id` -> `customers` ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules: uq_customer_addresses_text (one copy of an address per guest, case and whitespace-insensitive); trigger trg_customers_erased_forget_addresses on `customers` (function forget_erased_guest_addresses) deletes a guest's addresses when the guest is erased.
Added by: 1791330000001_guest-delivery-addresses.

## Restaurant module (continued): money, guests, stock and staff

### Business day and cash

#### `discounts`
Purpose: Coupons and offers a restaurant defines and applies to bills (percentage or flat amount, or buy-X-get-Y).
Key columns: `name`, `description`, `code` (VARCHAR(30), case-insensitive coupon code), `discount_type` (`percentage` | `amount`), `value` (NUMERIC(12,2) > 0), `max_discount_amount` (cap on a percentage discount), `min_order_amount`, `valid_from` / `valid_to` (DATE), `usage_limit` / `used_count`, `applies_to` (`all`, `dine_in`, `takeaway`, `delivery`, `online`), `status` (`active` | `inactive`), `offer_type` (`discount` | `buy_get`), `product_ids` / `category_ids` (BIGINT[]; NULL = every dish, otherwise only those lines count and are discounted), `weekdays` (SMALLINT[], 0 = Sunday … 6 = Saturday), `time_from` / `time_to` (TIME; `time_to` before `time_from` runs past midnight), `once_per_guest` (BOOLEAN), `buy_quantity` / `get_quantity` / `get_discount_percent` (buy X get Y: of every X+Y eligible dishes the Y cheapest are discounted by this %, 100 = free).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `created_by` / `updated_by` -> `users`.
Rules: one code per restaurant regardless of case (unique index `uq_discounts_restaurant_code` on `(restaurant_id, UPPER(code))`); `value > 0`, and `value <= 100` for a percentage (`chk_discount_percentage`); `valid_to >= valid_from` (`chk_discount_dates`); `usage_limit` and `max_discount_amount` positive when given; time window is both-or-neither (`chk_discount_time_window`); weekdays only 0-6 (`chk_discount_weekdays`); a `buy_get` offer must have buy, get and percent set (`chk_discount_buy_get`); buy/get quantities 1-50, get percent 1-100. "Once per guest" is enforced in code by looking up earlier bills of the guest with this discount (index `idx_bills_discount_id`).
Added by: 1789731798732_create-discounts-and-business-day; 1791340000003_coupon-rules.

#### `business_days`
Purpose: The trading day that sales, payments, cash and wastage are grouped by (not the calendar midnight), opened and closed per restaurant or outlet, with the cash-up at close.
Key columns: `outlet_id` (NULL = restaurant-wide / main counter), `business_date` (DATE), `opened_at` / `closed_at`, `opened_by` / `closed_by`, `day_status` (`open` | `closed`), `opening_cash` (>= 0), `closing_cash` (the cash the day accounts for: counted drawer plus drops to the safe minus paid-in top-ups; what reports compare with expected cash), `counted_cash` (what was physically in the drawer), `count_method` (`notes` | `total`), `count_denominations` (JSONB), `blind_count` (BOOLEAN), `first_count` (first figure typed, kept when the count was changed after the difference was shown), `expected_cash`, `cash_dropped`, `cash_paid_in`, `cash_variance`, `variance_reason` (VARCHAR(300)), `method_checks` (JSONB `[{method, system, reported, matched, note}]` for card/UPI reconciliation), `reopened_count`, `opening_notes` (VARCHAR(1000), the opener's note), `closing_notes` (TEXT, the closer's note; may list every unpaid bill), `notes` (the combined text, written too: opening + closing note plus a line per reopening).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `opened_by` / `closed_by` -> `users`. Referenced by bills, payments, refunds, credit notes, cash drawers/movements, advances, rider settlements and many order/kitchen tables.
Rules: one row per `(restaurant_id, outlet_id, business_date)` with NULL outlet treated as one key (`UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT`); only one open day per restaurant/outlet at a time (partial unique index `uq_business_days_one_open` on `(restaurant_id, COALESCE(outlet_id,0)) WHERE day_status = 'open'`); `opening_cash >= 0`, `closing_cash >= 0` when set. The allowed opening date (today, or yesterday until `restaurant_settings.day_cutoff_hour`), the variance threshold that forces a `variance_reason` (`cash_variance_threshold`) and blind counting (`cash_blind_count`) are settings enforced in code.
Added by: 1789731798732_create-discounts-and-business-day; 1790726900000_complete-operations-backlog (index `idx_business_days_outlet_status`); 1790810000000_cash-drawers-and-loyalty-rules (cash-up columns); 1791360000000_business-day-opening-and-closing-notes; 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_business_days_live`).

#### `cash_drawers`
Purpose: One cashier's stint at a till within a business day: opening float, payments taken while open, cash in/out, and the cashier's count at the end. The business day's own count remains the final word on the day's cash.
Key columns: `business_day_id`, `outlet_id`, `cashier_id` (the person answerable), `label`, `drawer_status` (`open` | `closed`), `opening_float` (0 to 99,999,999), `opened_at` / `opened_by`, `closed_at` / `closed_by`, `counted_cash`, `count_method` (`notes` | `total`), `count_denominations` (JSONB), `blind_count`, `first_count`, `expected_cash`, `cash_variance`, `variance_reason`, `notes`.
Relations: `business_day_id` -> `business_days` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets` (ON DELETE SET NULL); `cashier_id`, `opened_by`, `closed_by` -> `users`.
Rules: a closed drawer must have `closed_at` and `counted_cash` (CHECK); a cashier has at most one open drawer per business day (partial unique index `uq_cash_drawers_one_open` on `(business_day_id, cashier_id) WHERE drawer_status = 'open'`).
Added by: 1790810000000_cash-drawers-and-loyalty-rules.

#### `cash_movements`
Purpose: Cash taken into or out of a drawer outside sales: `paid_in` (float top-up from the safe), `petty_cash` (paid out for the restaurant, also recorded as an expense), `drop` (surplus moved to the safe mid-shift).
Key columns: `business_day_id`, `outlet_id`, `drawer_id` (the open shift drawer, when there is one), `movement_type`, `amount` (> 0, <= 99,999,999), `reason` (required), `expense_id`, `movement_status` (`recorded` | `cancelled`), `cancelled_at` / `cancelled_by` / `cancel_reason`, `approved_by` (manager whose PIN allowed it).
Relations: `business_day_id` -> `business_days` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets` (ON DELETE SET NULL); `drawer_id` -> `cash_drawers` (ON DELETE SET NULL); `expense_id` -> `expenses`; user columns -> `users`.
Rules: petty cash must point at its expense (`movement_type <> 'petty_cash' OR expense_id IS NOT NULL`), so expense reports and the drawer agree; a movement is never deleted, only cancelled, and a cancelled one must have `cancelled_at` (CHECK). Requires permission `cash.movements`; a cashier without it records one with a manager's PIN (`approved_by`).
Added by: 1790810000000_cash-drawers-and-loyalty-rules.

### Billing and payments

#### How bill money is computed
A bill is a snapshot: once raised its amounts are never recalculated from the menu. The columns on `bills` relate as follows:

```
subtotal                    sum of the billed order lines before any bill discount
                            (includes add-ons and any per-item "Packaging" modifier)
discount_amount           = coupon_discount + manual_discount_amount
                            + item_discount_amount + loyalty_discount
taxable_amount            = subtotal - discount_amount + service_charge + delivery_charge
tax_amount                = all GST (CGST + SGST) on the dishes, on the service charge
                            (service_charge_tax) and on the delivery charge
                            (delivery_charge_tax), plus cess (cess_amount)
total_amount              = taxable_amount + tax_amount + round_off
```

- Subtotal: the sum of `bill_items.line_subtotal` (the dish value before any bill discount). There is no packaging column on the bill: a packaging charge comes from `product_selling_rules.packaging_charge` (1790726800000) and is added in code to takeaway, delivery and online order lines as a `Packaging` modifier, so it sits inside the line value and is taxed with the dish.
- Discounts: `coupon_discount` (a `discounts` row, `discount_id` / `discount_code`), the till discount on the whole bill (`manual_discount_type` / `manual_discount_value` → `manual_discount_amount`), till discounts on single dishes (`item_discount_amount`, the sum of `bill_items.item_discount_amount`) and points redeemed (`loyalty_points_used` → `loyalty_discount`). `discount_reason`, `discount_note`, `discount_given_by` and `discount_approved_by` record who gave a till discount and the manager who approved it beyond the giver's role cap (`till_discount_limits`). A complimentary bill (`is_complimentary`) is every dish at 100% off with a mandatory `complimentary_reason`.
- Service charge: `service_charge_percentage` (0 when not charged; by default dine-in only, per `restaurant_settings.service_charge_order_types`) gives `service_charge`; it is spread across the dishes (`bill_items.service_charge_share`) and taxed at each dish's own GST rate. A guest's refusal sets `service_charge_waived` with a mandatory `service_charge_waive_reason`.
- Delivery charge: `delivery_charge` (before GST, taken from the order) is shared over the dishes like service charge (`bill_items.delivery_charge_share`) and taxed at each dish's rate; `delivery_charge_tax` is its GST, part of `tax_amount`. A manager can waive it (`delivery_charge_waived` + mandatory `delivery_charge_waive_reason`, `delivery_charge_waived_by`, `delivery_charge_approved_by`).
- Taxes: `tax_mode` (`inclusive` | `exclusive`) records how prices were read. `bill_taxes` holds one row per GST rate/cess pair: its taxable value is dishes + service charge share + delivery charge share for that rate, CGST + SGST equal the GST at that rate (an odd paisa goes to CGST), and the rows add up to the bill's tax to the paisa.
- Round-off: `round_off` (NUMERIC(6,2), may be negative) brings the total to the printed figure.
- Settlement: `paid_amount` is what payments have put toward the bill (tips are excluded: `payments.tip_amount` is never sales, GST, discount base or loyalty); `bill_status` moves `unpaid` → `partial` → `paid`, or `void` (with `void_reason`). `refunded_amount` is all money handed back (including a part payment handed back on an unsettled bill, `refunds.refund_kind = 'payment_reversal'`); `credited_amount` is only the money given back against the sale by credit note (`refund_kind = 'credit_note'`), which reduces sales and GST. Neither changes `total_amount`: the credit note, not an edit of the bill, carries the reduction.
- No database trigger computes these figures; they are written by the billing service in one transaction. The database guards them with non-negative CHECKs, the reason CHECKs and the `bill_taxes` / `credit_notes` arithmetic CHECKs described below.

#### `bills`
Purpose: The money document (GST tax invoice or bill of supply) for all or part of an order.
Key columns: `bill_number` (from `document_counters`, series per invoice prefix; may restart each financial year), `order_id`, `business_day_id`, `outlet_id`, the money columns above (`subtotal`, `discount_amount`, `coupon_discount`, `manual_discount_*`, `item_discount_amount`, `loyalty_points_used`, `loyalty_discount`, `service_charge_percentage`, `service_charge`, `service_charge_tax`, `service_charge_waived` / `_waive_reason`, `delivery_charge`, `delivery_charge_tax`, `delivery_charge_waived` / `_waive_reason` / `_waived_by` / `_approved_by`, `taxable_amount`, `tax_amount`, `cess_amount`, `tax_mode`, `round_off`, `total_amount`, `paid_amount`, `refunded_amount`, `credited_amount`), `bill_status` (`unpaid`, `partial`, `paid`, `void`), `void_reason`, `billed_at`, `settled_at`, `is_split` (one of several bills for one order), `is_complimentary` / `complimentary_reason` / `complimentary_note` / `complimentary_by` / `complimentary_approved_by`, `seller_gstin` and `seller_fssai` (as printed when raised, so reprints never change), `consume_stock` (whether this bill took its ingredients out of stock), `receipt_settings` (JSONB snapshot of print settings), `print_count`, `first_printed_at`, `last_printed_at`, `last_adjusted_at`, `account_customer_id` / `account_at` / `account_by` / `account_approved_by` / `account_note` (bill put on a guest's credit account), `buyer_pan` (CHAR(10), the buyer's PAN) and `pan_declaration` (the buyer has no PAN and signed Form 97): a bill over ₹2,00,000 takes no payment without one of them (trigger `trg_payments_buyer_pan`, under `payments`).
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `outlet_id` -> `outlets`; `business_day_id` -> `business_days`; `order_id` -> `orders`; `discount_id` -> `discounts`; `account_customer_id` -> `customers`; approver / giver columns -> `users`.
Rules: `bill_number` unique per restaurant; all amount columns `>= 0` except `round_off`; a complimentary bill needs a reason (`chk_bills_complimentary_reason`); a waived service charge needs a reason (`chk_bills_waive_reason`); a waived delivery charge needs a reason (`chk_bills_delivery_waiver`); `service_charge_percentage` 0-100; `buyer_pan` has a PAN's shape (five letters, four digits and a letter, CHECK) and never comes with `pan_declaration` (`chk_bills_pan_or_declaration`). That the bills of one order never bill more than was ordered is checked in code (split billing). Bill-stock reversal: `consume_stock` remembers whether the bill consumed stock at the time (the `auto_consume_stock` setting can change later), so voiding or returning an old bill puts back exactly the stock it took and never stock it did not. Partial indexes keep the bell cheap (`idx_bills_unsettled` on unpaid/partial bills), plus `idx_bills_account` (account bills), `idx_bills_discount_id`, `idx_bills_order_id` / `idx_bills_order`, `idx_bills_restaurant_number`.
Added by: 1789790367434_create-billing-and-payments; 1789802692848_split-bills-and-returns; 1789817853331_create-loyalty; 1789824100000_bill-stock-reversal; 1789904000000_create-notification-reads; 1789908000000_add-guest-insights; 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls; 1790727000000_service-readiness; 1790800000000_billing-series-and-guest-accounts; 1791340000002_bill-delivery-charge-fssai-upi-and-yearly-numbers; 1791340000003_coupon-rules; 1791972000000_restaurant-cash-limit-and-pan (`buyer_pan`, `pan_declaration`); 1791974000000_live-updates (live updates: `trg_bills_live`).

#### `bill_items`
Purpose: Which order lines, and how much of each, a bill covers (supports split bills), with the GST breakdown per line so HSN-wise GST adds up to the invoice.
Key columns: `order_item_id`, `quantity` (> 0), `line_subtotal` / `line_tax` / `line_total` (the dish before any bill discount), `item_discount_type` / `item_discount_value` / `item_discount_amount` (till discount on this dish), `discount_amount` (all discount falling on the line: its own plus its share of the bill's), `service_charge_share`, `delivery_charge_share`, `taxable_value`, `tax_percentage`, `cess_percentage`, `tax_amount`, `cess_amount`, `hsn_code`, `returned_quantity` (returned by credit note).
Relations: `bill_id` -> `bills` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `order_item_id` -> `order_items`.
Rules: one row per `(bill_id, order_item_id)`; `returned_quantity <= quantity` (`chk_bill_items_returned`); money columns non-negative.
Added by: 1789802692848_split-bills-and-returns; 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls; 1791340000002_bill-delivery-charge-fssai-upi-and-yearly-numbers.

#### `bill_taxes`
Purpose: The invoice's tax rate by rate: one row per GST rate and cess rate on a bill, read by the printed bill and the GST return.
Key columns: `tax_percentage`, `cess_percentage`, `items_taxable`, `service_charge_taxable`, `delivery_charge_taxable`, `taxable_value`, `cgst_amount`, `sgst_amount`, `service_charge_tax` (the service charge's part of CGST + SGST), `cess_amount`.
Relations: `restaurant_id` -> `restaurants` (CASCADE); `bill_id` -> `bills` (ON DELETE CASCADE).
Rules: unique `(bill_id, tax_percentage, cess_percentage)`; `taxable_value = items_taxable + service_charge_taxable + delivery_charge_taxable` (`bill_taxes_check`).
Added by: 1789913000000_billing-tax-invoice-and-till-controls; 1791340000002_bill-delivery-charge-fssai-upi-and-yearly-numbers.

#### `payments`
Purpose: Money taken against a bill.
Key columns: `bill_id`, `order_id`, `business_day_id`, `payment_method` (`cash`, `card`, `upi`, `online`, `wallet`, `other`, `advance`), `payment_type_id` (owner-named type under the method), `amount` (> 0, what goes toward the bill), `tip_amount` (>= 0, on top of `amount`; never sales, GST, discount base or loyalty), `tendered_amount`, `change_amount`, `transaction_ref`, `payment_status` (`success`, `failed`, `refunded`), `advance_id`, `paid_at`.
Relations: `bill_id` -> `bills` (ON DELETE CASCADE); `order_id` -> `orders`; `business_day_id` -> `business_days`; `payment_type_id` -> `payment_types` (ON DELETE RESTRICT); `advance_id` -> `customer_advances` (ON DELETE RESTRICT).
Rules: an `advance` payment must name its advance and only it may (`chk_payments_advance`: `(payment_method = 'advance') = (advance_id IS NOT NULL)`); an advance payment brings no new cash, so cash figures exclude it by method. Partial indexes `idx_payments_tips` (tip-bearing payments), `idx_payments_type`, `idx_payments_advance`.
- The cash limit (Income-tax Act 2025, section 186): trigger `trg_payments_cash_limit` (function `restaurant_cash_limit_payment()`, BEFORE INSERT, and BEFORE UPDATE OF `payment_method`, `amount`, `tip_amount`, `advance_id`, `payment_status`, so a correction to cash counts the cash it adds).
