Key columns: `name` (unique per business, ignoring case), `input_sku_id`, `active`.
Rules: trigger `weight_yield_templates_weighed` requires a weighed input SKU.
Added by: 1791430000001 weight-processing-yield-wastage.

#### `weight_yield_template_outputs`
Purpose: each expected cut on a template.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `template_id`, `output_sku_id`), `expected_percent` (more than 0, at most 100). The FK to the template uses ON DELETE CASCADE.
Rules:
- Trigger `weight_yield_template_outputs_weighed` requires a weighed output SKU.
- Deferred constraint trigger `weight_yield_template_outputs_total` refuses a template whose expected percentages add up to more than 100.
Added by: 1791430000001.

#### `weight_processing_batches`
Purpose: one cutting run, numbered with series kind `processing` and prefix `CP`.
Key columns: `batch_no` (unique), `series_id`, `location_id`, `template_id`, `input_sku_id`, `input_lot_id`, snapshots of the input (`input_label`, `input_unit`, `input_quantity`, `input_unit_cost`, `input_value`), `input_kg`, `output_kg`, `loss_kg`, `input_adjustment_id` -> `business_stock_adjustments`, `notes`, `cut_by`, `supplier_id` (FK to contacts: the supplier of the input batch's latest purchase), `cut_to_order`, `request_key`, `request_hash`.
Rules:
- CHECK: `output_kg + loss_kg = input_kg`.
- Append-only (`weight_append_only`).
Added by: 1791430000001; 1791600000000 (`cut_by`, `supplier_id`, `cut_to_order`).

#### `weight_processing_outputs`
Purpose: each cut produced by a batch.
Key columns: `batch_id`, `output_sku_id`, `label`, `unit`, `lot_id`, `quantity`, `kg`, `cost_share`, `unit_cost`, `expected_percent`, `adjustment_id`.
Rules:
- UNIQUE (`batch_id`, `output_sku_id`).
- Append-only.
Added by: 1791430000001.

#### `weight_wastage_entries`
Purpose: a stock write-off, valued at the lot's cost.
Key columns: `location_id`, `sku_id`, `lot_id`, `label`, `unit`, `quantity`, `kg` (NULL for counted items), `reason` (trimming, spoiled, unsold, drip_loss, damaged, other), `note`, `unit_cost`, `value`, `adjustment_id` (unique), `request_key`, `request_hash`.
Rules:
- CHECK: reason `other` needs a note.
- Append-only.
- There is no weighed-item trigger, so counted items can be written off too.
Added by: 1791430000001.

### Rates, packs, pickup orders and the day's weigh-up

#### `weight_rate_history`
Purpose: every change of a weighed item's selling price (the daily rate board): old, new, who, when.
Key columns: `sku_id`, `old_price`, `new_price` (both >= 0, 4 places), `changed_by`, `changed_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: append-only (trigger `weight_rate_history_append_only`); index `weight_rate_history_sku` (newest first).
Added by: 1791600000000.

#### `weight_pack_sizes`
Purpose: the weight of one pack of an item sold by the piece (loose stock repacked into pouches).
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `sku_id`), `pack_kg` (more than 0, at most 1000), `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Added by: 1791600000000.

#### `weight_pickup_orders`
Purpose: an order taken on the phone or WhatsApp for pickup at a time.
Key columns: `location_id`, `contact_id` (optional), `customer_name` (not blank), `phone`, `pickup_at`, `status` (open, ready, collected, cancelled), `notes`, `document_id` (the bill), `ready_at`, `closed_at`, `cancel_reason`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`, `updated_by`.
Relations: composite FKs to `business_locations`, `business_contacts` and `business_documents`.
Rules: CHECKs collected ⇔ bill, cancelled ⇔ reason, and closed ⇔ collected or cancelled; idempotent by `request_key` (unique per business); partial index `weight_pickup_orders_open` (open and ready orders by pickup time).
Added by: 1791600000000.

#### `weight_pickup_order_lines`
Purpose: the items on a pickup order, by weight or by amount ("₹200 worth"); the counter weighs them.
Key columns: `order_id`, `sku_id`, `label`, `unit`, `quantity` (> 0), `amount` (> 0), `note`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `weight_pickup_orders`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECK a quantity or an amount is given; index `weight_pickup_order_lines_order`.
Added by: 1791600000000.

#### `weight_day_closes`
Purpose: the end-of-day weigh-up at a location: what was left, against what the books expected.
Key columns: `location_id`, `day`, `lines` (JSONB: expected, counted, difference, value and reason per item), `short_kg`, `short_value`, `over_kg`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `location_id`) -> `business_locations`.
Rules: append-only (trigger `weight_day_closes_append_only`); idempotent by `request_key` (unique per business); index `weight_day_closes_day` (newest first).
Added by: 1791600000000.

## Pharmacy, clinic & lab

Linked section: [pharmacy, clinic and lab](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md#pharmacy-clinic-and-lab).

Modules `pharmacy` (type pharmacy; needs sales, batches, contacts), `patients` (type healthcare; needs contacts, appointments) and `lab` (healthcare; needs patients, sales). `pharmacy` and `patients` are on by default; `lab` is off by default and recommended for healthcare. Permissions `pharmacy.*` (view, create, edit, approve, export, print), `patients.*` (view, create, edit, **view_clinical**, **edit_clinical**, export, print) and `lab.*` (view, create, edit, approve, print, export). The role templates `pharmacist` (pharmacy), `doctor`, `lab_technician` and `pathologist` (healthcare) are given to their type only (`business_role_templates.business_types`).

Every table here is scoped by `business_id` with composite foreign keys. "Append-only" means trigger function `health_append_only()`: a row is kept as written (only `created_at` may be corrected) and never deleted. The tables come from 1791620004000_pharmacy-clinic-lab, except a pharmacy branch's own licences (1791630000000), doctors' fees, OPD sessions, leave and the token queue (1791950408000), and lab report versions and quality control (1791950409000); each entry names its migrations.

### Shared: settings, numbers, signatories, files

#### `health_settings`
Purpose: the settings of a pharmacy or a clinic, one row per business, made on first save.
Key columns: PK `business_id`; drug licences `drug_licence_20`, `drug_licence_21` (retail), `drug_licence_20b`, `drug_licence_21b` (wholesale), `drug_licence_20f` (Schedule X retail); retention years `h1_retention_years` (3), `x_retention_years` (2), `prescription_retention_years` (2), `clinical_retention_years` (3), each 1–30; `rx_scan_required` (default true: a Schedule H / H1 / X sale needs a photo or scan of the prescription); `near_expiry_days` (7–365, default 90; the pharmacy's own, in place of `business_settings.near_expiry_days`); lab `lab_name`, `lab_registration`, `report_footer`; `consent_version` (default `v1`) and `consent_notice` (50 to 4000 characters, with a default text); `referral_commission_enabled`, `referral_commission_percent` (0–50); `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Rules: ranges by CHECK. A branch with its own licence numbers prints those from `pharmacy_location_licences`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `health_counters`
Purpose: running numbers per business, taken inside the posting: patient `P00001`, visit `OP00001`, lab order `LAB00001`, sample barcode `S0000001`, debit note `DN00001`.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `kind`), `kind` (patient, visit, lab_order, sample, debit_note), `next_number` (> 0).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `health_signatories`
Purpose: the people who sign, each a member of the business who signs with their own PIN: a registered pharmacist (Schedule X), a doctor (their registration number on the prescription) and a pathologist (lab reports).
Key columns: `user_id`, `kind` (pharmacist, doctor, pathologist), `name` (as registered, not blank), `qualification`, `registration_no` (not blank), `council`, `resource_id` (a doctor's appointment calendar), `active`, `created_by`, `updated_by`.
Relations: `user_id` -> `users`; (`business_id`, `resource_id`) -> `business_resources`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, kind, user); one active signatory per calendar (partial unique index `health_signatories_resource`).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `health_files`
Purpose: a photo or scan of a prescription, kept in `health/<business>/` under the private upload folder (`PRIVATE_UPLOADS_DIR`, `backend/private_uploads` when it is not set).
Key columns: `kind` (`prescription`), `stored_name` (UNIQUE), `file_name`, `mime_type` (JPEG, PNG, WebP, PDF; the bytes are checked by the API), `size_bytes` (> 0), `uploaded_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: referenced by `pharmacy_prescriptions.file_id` (one prescription per file).
Added by: 1791620004000.

### Pharmacy

#### `pharmacy_location_licences`
Purpose: a pharmacy branch's own drug licence numbers (each premises is licensed on its own). A location without a row prints the business-wide numbers from `health_settings`.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `location_id`), `drug_licence_20`, `drug_licence_21`, `drug_licence_20b`, `drug_licence_21b`, `drug_licence_20f`, `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `location_id`) -> `business_locations`.
Added by: 1791630000000 business-multi-location.

#### `pharmacy_drugs`
Purpose: the drug master, one row per SKU.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `sku_id`), `composition` (the salts, not blank) and the generated `composition_key` (lower case, single spaces: substitutes share it), `strength`, `form` (tablet, capsule, syrup, suspension, injection, ointment, cream, drops, inhaler, powder, gel, solution, device, other), `manufacturer`, `schedule` (none, G, H, H1, X, narcotic), `units_per_strip`, `strips_per_box` (each 1–1000), `loose_sale` (a strip may be cut and sold by the tablet), `cold_chain`, `storage`, `updated_by` (NOT NULL), `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECK a loose sale needs `units_per_strip`; indexes `pharmacy_drugs_composition` and `pharmacy_drugs_schedule` (scheduled drugs only).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `pharmacy_prescriptions`
Purpose: the prescription a sale of Schedule H / H1 / X medicines was made on; one per bill.
Key columns: `document_id` (the sale, unique), `contact_id`, `patient_name` (not blank), `patient_phone`, `patient_age` (0–130), `patient_address`, `doctor_name` and `doctor_reg_no` (not blank), `doctor_address`, `prescribed_on`, `file_id` (the scan, unique), `signatory_id` (the pharmacist under whose supervision it was sold, if signed), `retain_until`, `created_by`.
Relations: composite FKs to `business_documents`, `business_contacts`, `health_files` and `health_signatories`.
Rules: CHECK `prescribed_on` is not after the day after it was recorded; append-only (trigger `pharmacy_prescriptions_append_only`); index `pharmacy_prescriptions_time`. Constraint trigger `pharmacy_scheduled_sale_check` on `business_document_lines` (deferred to commit) refuses a sale line of an H / H1 / X / narcotic drug, while the pharmacy module is on, unless the sale has its prescription here.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `pharmacy_register_entries`
Purpose: the H1, Schedule X and narcotic registers: one entry per sold line, written with the sale.
Key columns: `register` (H1, X, narcotic), `prescription_id`, `document_id`, `line_id` (unique), `sku_id`, `drug_name`, `composition`, `quantity` (> 0), `unit`, `lot_code`, `expires_on`, `patient_name`, `patient_address`, `doctor_name`, `doctor_reg_no`, `doctor_address`, `signatory_id`, `retain_until`, `created_by`.
Relations: composite FKs to `pharmacy_prescriptions`, `business_documents`, `catalog_skus` and `health_signatories`.
Rules: CHECK an X or narcotic entry names the pharmacist (`signatory_id`); append-only (trigger `pharmacy_register_entries_append_only`); index `pharmacy_register_entries_time` (register, newest first).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `pharmacy_recalls`
Purpose: a batch recall: every sale of the batch can be traced, and while blocked it cannot be sold or moved (it can still go back to the distributor or be written off).
Key columns: `sku_id`, `lot_code` and `reason` (both not blank), `reference` (the regulator's or maker's alert number), `blocked`, `status` (open, closed), `created_by`, `closed_by`, `closed_at`, `close_note`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECKs closed ⇔ closed_at and a closed recall is not blocked; one open recall per batch (partial unique index `pharmacy_recalls_open`). Trigger `pharmacy_recalled_lot_check` on `business_stock_movements` refuses any outgoing movement of a blocked batch except a supplier return or a correction (stock adjustment).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `pharmacy_debit_notes`
Purpose: expired, near-expiry, broken or recalled stock sent back to a distributor. The stock leaves through a supplier return on the purchase that brought the batch, or is written off when there is none; the distributor's credit note closes the debit note.
Key columns: `note_no` (`DN00001`, unique per business), `contact_id` (the distributor), `location_id`, `reason` (expired, near_expiry, breakage, recall, other), `status` (sent, credited), `claimed` (supplier returns at the purchase price with GST), `written_off`, `credit_note_no`, `credit_note_date`, `credited_amount`, `notes`, `request_key` (unique per business), `request_hash`, `created_by`, `credited_by`, `credited_at`.
Relations: composite FKs to `business_contacts` and `business_locations`.
Rules: CHECK credited ⇔ credit time, credit note number and credited amount are all set; amounts >= 0; index `pharmacy_debit_notes_time`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `pharmacy_debit_note_lines`
Purpose: one batch on a debit note, and how it left.
Key columns: `debit_note_id`, `lot_id`, `sku_id`, `label`, `lot_code`, `expires_on`, `quantity` (> 0), `unit`, `value` (>= 0), `return_id` (the core supplier return in `business_returns`) or `adjustment_id` (the write-off in `business_stock_adjustments`).
Relations: composite FKs to `pharmacy_debit_notes` and `catalog_skus`; `lot_id`, `return_id` and `adjustment_id` carry no FK.
Rules: CHECK exactly one of `return_id` and `adjustment_id`; append-only (trigger `pharmacy_debit_note_lines_append_only`); index `pharmacy_debit_note_lines_note`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

### Clinic

#### `health_patients`
Purpose: a customer contact as a patient.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `contact_id`), `patient_no` (`P00001`, unique per business), `date_of_birth`, `age_years` (0–130, the age given at registration when the date of birth is not known), `sex` (female, male, other), `guardian_name`, `emergency_phone`, `registered_by`, `updated_by`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`; the target of the patient FK on consents, clinical details, visits, tokens and lab orders.
Rules: CHECK a date of birth or an age is given.
Added by: 1791620004000.

A patient's consents (treatment, reports on WhatsApp or e-mail, reminders, given on the clinic's own consent notice) are rows of [`business_consents`](#business_consents), which 1791977000000 made of the table `health_consents` (1791620004000), its rows kept with their ids.

#### `health_patient_clinical`
Purpose: the clinical side of a patient, read only by clinical roles (`patients.view_clinical`).
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `contact_id`), `blood_group` (A+, A-, B+, B-, AB+, AB-, O+, O-), `allergies`, `conditions` (long-term), `current_medicines`, `updated_by` (NOT NULL), `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `health_patients`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `health_doctor_profiles`
Purpose: a doctor's consultation terms: the service billed for a first visit, the one billed for a follow-up inside the follow-up window (none: the follow-up is free), the length of an appointment slot, and the doctor's share of their fees.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `resource_id`), `speciality`, `consultation_sku_id`, `follow_up_sku_id`, `follow_up_days` (0–90), `slot_minutes` (5–120, default 10), `share_percent` (0–100), `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `resource_id`) -> `business_resources` (the doctor's calendar); both services -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECK a follow-up service needs a consultation service.
Added by: 1791950408000 clinic-doctors-tokens.

#### `health_doctor_sessions`
Purpose: a doctor's OPD sessions; a doctor may sit mornings and evenings (two rows on one day).
Key columns: `resource_id`, `dow` (0–6), `starts`, `ends` (after `starts`), `max_patients` (1–500; a doctor's token cap for a day is the sum over that day's sessions, and a session with none, NULL, leaves the day uncapped).
Relations: (`business_id`, `resource_id`) -> `business_resources`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, doctor, weekday, start).
Added by: 1791950408000.

#### `health_doctor_leave`
Purpose: a doctor's leave, from one date to another, with a reason; cancellable.
Key columns: `resource_id`, `from_date`, `to_date`, `reason`, `created_by`, `cancelled_at`, `cancelled_by`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `resource_id`) -> `business_resources`.
Rules: CHECK `to_date >= from_date` and at most 367 days (`to_date - from_date <= 366`); partial index `health_doctor_leave_days` on leave not cancelled.
Added by: 1791950408000.

#### `health_tokens`
Purpose: the day's token queue at the front desk: one number per doctor per day (or for the clinic, when no doctor is chosen yet), called in turn.
Key columns: `location_id`, `resource_id` (the doctor; NULL = the clinic), `day`, `token_no` (> 0), `contact_id` (the patient), `appointment_id`, `visit_id`, `priority`, `status` (waiting, called, in_consultation, done, skipped, cancelled), `note`, `issued_by`, `issued_at`, `called_at`, `called_count`, `started_at`, `done_at`.
Relations: composite FKs to `business_locations`, `business_resources`, `health_patients`, `business_appointments` and `health_visits`.
Rules: a number is given once per location, doctor (or clinic) and day (unique index `health_tokens_number`); one live token per appointment (`health_tokens_appointment`, cancelled ones aside); a patient waits once per doctor at a time (`health_tokens_waiting`, on waiting, called and in-consultation tokens); index `health_tokens_day`.
Added by: 1791950408000; 1791974000000 (live updates: `health_tokens_live`).

#### `health_visits`
Purpose: a consultation, the medical record of one visit.
Key columns: `visit_no` (`OP00001`, unique per business), `contact_id` (the patient), `appointment_id` (one visit each), `resource_id` (the doctor's calendar), `doctor_signatory_id`, `location_id` (the branch: the doctor's, else the branch the reception works at), `status` (open, completed); vitals `bp_systolic` (40–300), `bp_diastolic` (20–200), `pulse` (20–250), `temperature_c` (30–45), `spo2` (50–100), `resp_rate` (4–80), `weight_kg` (0.5–400), `height_cm` (20–250); `complaints`, `examination`, `diagnosis`, `icd10_code` (ICD-10 shape), `advice`, `follow_up_on`, `referred_to` (to whom the doctor refers the patient: a specialist, a hospital; up to 200) and `referral_reason` (up to 1000), `document_id` (the bill); how the consultation was charged, written once when the visit is billed (a free follow-up with nothing else to bill is recorded with no bill): `fee_kind` (consultation, follow_up, free_follow_up) and `fee_sku_id` (so the doctor's earnings stay as billed when fees change); `created_by`, `updated_by`, `completed_by`, `completed_at`.
Relations: composite FKs to `health_patients`, `business_appointments`, `business_resources`, `health_signatories`, `business_documents`, `business_locations` (`health_visits_location_fk`) and `catalog_skus` (`health_visits_fee_sku_fk`).
Rules: CHECK completed ⇔ completed_at; CHECK `health_visits_referral` (a referral's reason only with someone it is to); trigger `health_visit_fixed`: a consultation is never deleted, and once completed it is fixed: only its bill and its fee may be added, each once (a correction is a note in `health_visit_notes`). Indexes `health_visits_patient`, `health_visits_follow_up` (partial) and `health_visits_location` (business, location, newest first).
Added by: 1791620004000; 1791950407000 visit-location (`location_id`); 1791950408000 (`fee_kind`, `fee_sku_id`; the trigger lets the fee be written once); 1791976000000 clinic-lab-workspaces (`referred_to`, `referral_reason`, `health_visits_referral`).

#### `health_visit_notes`
Purpose: dated notes added to a consultation after it was completed.
Key columns: `visit_id`, `body` (not blank, up to 2000), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `visit_id`) -> `health_visits`.
Rules: append-only (trigger `health_visit_notes_append_only`).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `health_prescription_items`
Purpose: the doctor's prescription lines on a consultation.
Key columns: `visit_id`, `drug_name` (generic name, not blank), `strength`, `dose`, `frequency`, `duration`, `instructions`, `sort_order`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `visit_id`) -> `health_visits`.
Rules: trigger `health_prescription_open` refuses inserts, changes and deletes once the visit is completed; index `health_prescription_items_visit`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

### Lab

#### `lab_tests`
Purpose: a test (one or many parameters: CBC, LFT, lipid profile) or a profile (several tests sold together). A test may name the catalogue service it is billed as.
Key columns: `code` (`^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9_-]{0,29}$`, unique per business), `name` (not blank), `kind` (test, profile), `sku_id`, `sample_type` (blood, serum, plasma, urine, stool, swab, sputum, other), `container`, `method`, `turnaround_hours` (1–720, default 24), `active`, `created_by`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: one test per service (partial unique index `lab_tests_sku`).
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `lab_profile_tests`
Purpose: the tests a profile is made of.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `profile_id`, `test_id`), `sort_order`.
Relations: both `profile_id` and `test_id` -> `lab_tests` (composite).
Rules: CHECK a profile does not contain itself.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `lab_parameters`
Purpose: a parameter a test reports.
Key columns: `test_id`, `name` (not blank; unique per test), `unit`, `result_type` (numeric, text), `decimals` (0–4, default 1), `sort_order`, `active`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `test_id`) -> `lab_tests`; the target of reference ranges, results and QC materials.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `lab_reference_ranges`
Purpose: a parameter's reference range by sex and age band (in days, so a newborn's range fits).
Key columns: `parameter_id`, `sex` (any, female, male), `age_from_days` (>= 0), `age_to_days` (up to 54750, about 150 years), `low`, `high`, `critical_low`, `critical_high`, `normal_text` (the normal answer to a text result, "Negative").
Relations: (`business_id`, `parameter_id`) -> `lab_parameters`.
Rules: CHECKs the age band is not empty, `high >= low`, and a range has a low, a high or a normal text; index `lab_reference_ranges_parameter`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `lab_orders`
Purpose: a lab order for a patient, from ordering to the approved report.
Key columns: `order_no` (`LAB00001`, unique per business), `location_id`, `contact_id` (the patient), `visit_id`, `referred_by`, `priority` (routine, urgent), `status` (ordered → collected → received → in_process → resulted → approved, or cancelled), `document_id` (the bill), an outside lab's `outside_lab`, `outside_ref_no`, `outside_sent_at`, `outside_charge` and `referral_commission` (what it pays back, when the setting allows), `approved_by`, `approved_signatory_id`, `approved_at`, `cancel_reason`, `notes`, `request_key` (unique per business), `request_hash`, `created_by`; amendment `version` (how many times the report has been signed; 0 before the first), `amend_reason`, `amend_requested_by`, `amend_requested_at`.
Relations: composite FKs to `business_locations`, `health_patients`, `health_visits`, `business_documents` and `health_signatories`; `amend_requested_by` -> `users`.
Rules: CHECKs approved ⇔ approved_at, a cancelled order has a reason, the outside-lab columns only with an outside lab, and `lab_orders_amending` (an amendment reason is held only while the report is reopened, not approved). Indexes `lab_orders_time`, `lab_orders_visit` and `lab_orders_open` (orders not yet approved or cancelled).
Added by: 1791620004000; 1791950409000 lab-report-versions-qc (the amendment columns); 1791976000000 (live updates: `lab_orders_live`).

#### `lab_samples`
Purpose: one sample per sample type of an order, with its barcode.
Key columns: `order_id`, `barcode` (`^[A-Z0-9-]{4,20}$`, unique per business), `sample_type`, `container`, `status` (awaited, collected, received, rejected), `collected_at` / `collected_by`, `received_at` / `received_by`, `reject_reason`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `lab_orders`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, order, sample type); CHECKs a collected or received sample has `collected_at`, a received one `received_at`, a rejected one a reason.
Added by: 1791620004000; 1791976000000 (live updates: `lab_samples_live`).

#### `lab_order_tests`
Purpose: a test on an order, the sample it is run on, and the profile it came with, if any.
Key columns: `order_id`, `test_id` (once per order), `profile_id`, `sample_id`.
Relations: composite FKs to `lab_orders`, `lab_tests` (test and profile) and `lab_samples`.
Added by: 1791620004000.

#### `lab_results`
Purpose: one value per parameter of an ordered test, with its flag and the unit and range it was judged by.
Key columns: `order_id`, `order_test_id`, `parameter_id`, `value_numeric` or `value_text` (exactly one), `unit`, `range_text`, `flag` (normal, low, high, critical_low, critical_high, abnormal, none), `entered_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: composite FKs to `lab_orders`, `lab_order_tests` and `lab_parameters`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, ordered test, parameter); trigger `lab_results_open` refuses any change once the order is approved or cancelled.
Added by: 1791620004000; 1791976000000 (live updates: `lab_results_live`).

#### `lab_report_versions`
Purpose: every signed report, kept as it was printed (results, ranges, flags, who signed), numbered 1, 2, …. A correction after signing is an amendment: the pathologist reopens the report with a reason, corrects it and signs again; the earlier version can still be printed, marked superseded.
Key columns: `order_id`, `version` (> 0), `results` (jsonb), `amend_reason` (NULL exactly for version 1), `signatory_id`, `approved_by`, `approved_at`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `lab_orders`; (`business_id`, `signatory_id`) -> `health_signatories`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, order, version); CHECK `(version = 1) = (amend_reason IS NULL)`; trigger `lab_report_versions_fixed` (function `lab_report_version_fixed`): never deleted or changed, except that both time stamps may be moved by the same amount.
Added by: 1791950409000 lab-report-versions-qc.

#### `lab_qc_materials`
Purpose: a quality-control material: a control (its lot and level) for one parameter, with the target mean and SD the lab works to (from the insert sheet or its own runs).
Key columns: `parameter_id`, `name` and `lot_no` (both not blank), `level` (default `Level 1`), `mean`, `sd` (> 0), `expires_on`, `active`, `created_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `parameter_id`) -> `lab_parameters`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, parameter, lot, level).
Added by: 1791950409000.

#### `lab_qc_runs`
Purpose: a QC run on a material: the value measured, how far it is from the mean (`z`), the Westgard rules it broke and the outcome. A failed run is followed by a corrective action and a new run, never edited. The code grades each run (1-2s warns; 1-3s, 2-2s, R-4s, 4-1s and 10x reject), and while a parameter's latest run today is rejected, patient reports with that parameter cannot be signed.
Key columns: `material_id`, `parameter_id`, `value`, `z`, `rules` (text[]), `outcome` (accepted, warning, rejected), `note`, `corrective_action`, `run_at`, `entered_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `material_id`) -> `lab_qc_materials`; (`business_id`, `parameter_id`) -> `lab_parameters`.
Rules: CHECK a rejected run names at least one rule; append-only (trigger `lab_qc_runs_fixed`); indexes `lab_qc_runs_material` and `lab_qc_runs_parameter` (newest first).
Added by: 1791950409000.

## Hardware

A hardware, paint and building-material shop's own tables (type `hardware`), added by 1791961000000 quotations-and-hardware; paint tinting, the calculators' workings and a sales order's fitter by 1791979050000 licences-hardware. How they are used: [BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md › Hardware, paints & building materials](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md#hardware-paints--building-materials).

### Sites

Module `hardware_sites` ("Sites", optional, hardware only, on) needs contacts and sales; permissions `hardware_sites.view` and `hardware_sites.edit`.

#### `hardware_sites`
Purpose: a contractor's or builder's site, under one customer.
Key columns: `contact_id`, `name`, `address`, `contact_person`, `phone`, `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules:
- Unique index `hardware_sites_name` (business, customer, lower(name)).
- Trigger `hardware_sites_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`): the contact is a customer.
- Trigger `hardware_sites_guard`: never deleted; the customer and who made it never change.
Added by: 1791961000000; 1791977000000 (`hardware_sites_guard` lets an erasure's change through: `business_privacy_erasing`).

#### `hardware_document_sites`
Purpose: the site a sale went to.
Key columns: `document_id` (primary key with the business), `site_id`, `set_by`, `set_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `business_documents`; (`business_id`, `site_id`) -> `hardware_sites`.
Rules: trigger `hardware_document_sites_check` (function `hardware_site_link_check`): the document is a sale to the site's own customer. Index `hardware_document_sites_site`.
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_quotation_sites`
Purpose: the site of a quotation.
Key columns: `quotation_id` (primary key with the business), `site_id`, `set_by`, `set_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `quotation_id`) -> `business_quotations`; (`business_id`, `site_id`) -> `hardware_sites`.
Rules: trigger `hardware_quotation_sites_check`: the quotation's customer owns the site, and the row changes (or goes) only while the quotation is open. Index `hardware_quotation_sites_site`.
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_order_sites`
Purpose: the site of a sales order; every invoice its dispatches raise goes there.
Key columns: `order_id` (primary key with the business), `site_id`, `set_by`, `set_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `wholesale_orders`; (`business_id`, `site_id`) -> `hardware_sites`.
Rules:
- Trigger `hardware_order_sites_check`: the order's customer owns the site.
- Trigger `wholesale_dispatches_hardware_site` on `wholesale_dispatches` (function `hardware_dispatch_site`): a dispatch's invoice is put on the order's site.
- Index `hardware_order_sites_site`.
Added by: 1791961000000.

### Today's rates

Module `hardware_rates` ("Today's rates", optional, hardware only, on) needs catalog; it has no permissions of its own (`catalog.view` and `catalog.edit`).

#### `hardware_rate_items`
Purpose: the items on a hardware shop's rate board, and when each one's price last changed.
Key columns: `sku_id` (primary key with the business), `previous_price`, `price_changed_at`, `added_by`, `added_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECK `previous_price` and `price_changed_at` are both set or both empty; trigger `catalog_skus_hardware_rate` on `catalog_skus` (function `hardware_rate_item_changed`), after a change of `price`, stamps the price before and the time.
Added by: 1791961000000.

An item's earlier rates are its `catalog.price_changed` rows of `business_audit`, read through the partial index `business_audit_price_changes` (business, entity, id DESC) (1791979050000).

### Fitters' commission

Module `fitter_commission` ("Fitters' commission", optional, hardware only, off and recommended) needs catalog and sales; permissions `fitter_commission.view`, `.create`, `.edit` and `.approve` (pay). Commission lines, reversals and payouts are append-only: trigger function `hardware_append_only` refuses any update or delete (SQL state 23514); a bill's commission row changes only its fitter, until the commission is paid (`hardware_fitter_bills_guard`).

#### `hardware_fitters`
Purpose: a painter, plumber, electrician, mason or carpenter who brings customers.
Key columns: `name`, `trade` (painter, plumber, electrician, mason, carpenter, other), `phone`, `upi_id`, `area`, `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Rules: CHECK the name is not blank; CHECK `upi_id` looks like name@bank; unique index `hardware_fitters_phone` (business, phone) where a phone is given.
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_fitter_rules`
Purpose: what a bill line earns: a percent of its value before GST, or an amount per unit of one item.
Key columns: `category_id` or `sku_id` (neither: every item), `percent` (more than 0, up to 50) or `per_unit` (more than 0), `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `category_id`) -> `catalog_categories`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: CHECK not both a category and an item; CHECK exactly one of percent and per unit; CHECK per unit only for one item; unique index `hardware_fitter_rules_one_active` (business, category or 0, item or 0) where active.
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_fitter_bills`
Purpose: the fitter of a bill and the commission it earned when it was posted.
Key columns: `document_id` (primary key with the business), `fitter_id`, `commission` (0 or more), `created_by`, `created_at`, `changed_by`/`changed_at` (the fitter last changed).
Relations: (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `business_documents`; (`business_id`, `fitter_id`) -> `hardware_fitters`.
Rules: trigger `hardware_fitter_bills_guard`: never deleted; only the fitter changes (with `changed_by` and `changed_at`), and not once the commission on the bill is paid (function `hardware_fitter_bill_paid(business, document)`: payouts not reversed settle a fitter's commission oldest bill first, less what returns took back). CHECK `hardware_fitter_bills_changed`: who and when together. Index `hardware_fitter_bills_fitter` (business, fitter, date).
Added by: 1791961000000; 1791979050000 (`changed_by`, `changed_at`, `hardware_fitter_bill_paid`; `hardware_fitter_bills_guard` replaces trigger `hardware_fitter_bills_append_only`).

#### `hardware_fitter_lines`
Purpose: each bill line that earned commission, with the rule's rate as it was.
Key columns: `line_id` (primary key with the business), `document_id`, `rule_id`, `percent` or `per_unit`, `quantity` (stock units), `net`, `commission`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `line_id`) -> `business_document_lines`; (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `hardware_fitter_bills`; (`business_id`, `rule_id`) -> `hardware_fitter_rules`.
Rules: CHECK the commission is more than 0 and not above the net; CHECK exactly one of percent and per unit; append-only (trigger `hardware_fitter_lines_append_only`).
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_fitter_reversals`
Purpose: commission taken back by goods returned on a bill.
Key columns: `return_line_id` (unique), `return_id`, `line_id`, `document_id`, `quantity`, `amount` (0 or more), `created_at`.
Relations: `return_line_id` -> `business_return_lines`; (`business_id`, `return_id`) -> `business_returns`; (`business_id`, `line_id`) -> `hardware_fitter_lines`.
Rules: written by trigger `business_return_lines_hardware_fitter` (function `hardware_fitter_reverse_on_return`) after each return line of a line that earned: the line's commission × quantity back so far ÷ quantity sold, rounded to the paisa, less what earlier returns took back. Append-only (trigger `hardware_fitter_reversals_append_only`); index `hardware_fitter_reversals_document`.
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_fitter_payouts`
Purpose: money paid to a fitter, carried by a paid expense.
Key columns: `fitter_id`, `expense_id` (unique), `amount` (more than 0), `notes`, `request_key` (unique per business), `request_hash`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `fitter_id`) -> `hardware_fitters`; (`business_id`, `expense_id`) -> `business_expenses`.
Rules: append-only (trigger `hardware_fitter_payouts_append_only`); a payout counts while its expense is not reversed; index `hardware_fitter_payouts_fitter` (business, fitter, date).
Added by: 1791961000000.

#### `hardware_order_fitters`
Purpose: the fitter a sales order names; every invoice its dispatches raise earns them their commission.
Key columns: `order_id` (primary key with the business), `fitter_id`, `set_by`, `set_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `wholesale_orders`; (`business_id`, `fitter_id`) -> `hardware_fitters`.
Added by: 1791979050000.

### Material calculators

Module `hardware_calculators` ("Material calculators", optional, hardware only, on) needs catalog; no permissions of its own (`catalog.view`). The arithmetic is the server's (`hardwareCalculatorMaths.js`).

#### `hardware_calculations`
Purpose: a calculator's working kept with a quotation (with the revision it was added in) or a bill: what was typed, the assumptions, the result, the materials and the lines it put on.
Key columns: `quotation_id` and `revision`, or `document_id` (exactly one), `kind` (tiles, paint, brickwork, concrete, plaster, steel), `working` (JSONB object: `inputs`, `assumptions`, `result`, `materials`, `added`), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `quotation_id`) -> `business_quotations`; (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `business_documents`.
Rules: partial indexes `hardware_calculations_quotation` and `hardware_calculations_document`; trigger `hardware_calculations_fixed`: never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791979050000.

### Paint tinting

Module `hardware_tinting` ("Paint tinting", optional, hardware only, off and recommended) needs catalog, sales and inventory; permissions `hardware_tinting.view`, `.create` (tinted lines; moving jobs on the board) and `.edit` (shades and the charge). An item's base is kept in `catalog_skus.attributes` (key `paintBase`; no table).

#### `hardware_tint_settings`
Purpose: the tinting charge: the service item billed beside a tinted line, one for each tin.
Key columns: `business_id` (primary key), `charge_sku_id` (NULL: none), `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `charge_sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Added by: 1791979050000.

#### `hardware_shades`
Purpose: a paint maker's shade.
Key columns: `maker`, `code`, `name`, `card`, `base`, `formula` (JSONB array of `{ colourant, ml }` for one litre of base), `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Rules: unique index `hardware_shades_code` (business, lower(maker), upper(code)); trigger `hardware_shades_guard`: never deleted; who added it never changes.
Added by: 1791979050000.

#### `hardware_quotation_tints`
Purpose: the shade a quotation's line is tinted to.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `line_id`), `quotation_id`, `shade_id`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `line_id`) -> `business_quotation_lines` (deleted with it when an open quotation's lines are replaced); (`business_id`, `quotation_id`) -> `business_quotations`; (`business_id`, `shade_id`) -> `hardware_shades`.
Rules: index `hardware_quotation_tints_quotation`.
Added by: 1791979050000.

#### `hardware_tint_jobs`
Purpose: a bill's tinted line on the tinting board.
Key columns: `location_id`, `document_id`, `line_id` (unique), `sku_id` (the base), `shade_id`, `quantity` (stock units), `status` (queued, tinted, handed_over, taken_back), `tinted_by`/`tinted_at`, `handed_over_by`/`handed_over_at`, `take_back_key` (the take-back return's request key), `approved_by`, `take_back_reason`, `taken_back_by`/`taken_back_at`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, …) -> `business_locations`, `business_documents`, `business_document_lines`, `catalog_skus`, `hardware_shades`.
Rules:
- CHECKs: each step's who and when together; handed over only once tinted; each status with its own stamps; a take-back's key, approver, reason, who and when together.
- Indexes `hardware_tint_jobs_board` (business, status, date) and `hardware_tint_jobs_document`.
- Trigger `hardware_tint_jobs_guard`: never deleted; the bill, line, base, shade, quantity and who made it never change; queued to tinted to handed over one step at a time, or taken back; a job taken back no longer changes; the time it was tinted stays.
- Trigger `business_return_lines_hardware_tint` on `business_return_lines` (function `hardware_tint_line_return`): a return line of a tinted line is refused unless its return carries the job's take-back key and puts nothing back in stock.
Added by: 1791979050000.

## Seeds, fertilisers & pesticides

A krishi kendra's own tables (type `agri_inputs`), added by 1791962000000 licences-and-agri-inputs. Module `agri` ("Seeds, fertilisers & pesticides", optional, agri_inputs only, on) needs catalog, contacts, sales, inventory and batches; permissions `agri.view`, `agri.create` (type the PoS stock) and `agri.edit` (farmers' details). Item classification is kept in `catalog_skus.attributes` (keys `inputKind`, `subsidised`, `seedClass`, `seedCrop`, `seedVariety`, `molecules`, `formulation`, `netKg`; no table). How they are used: [BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md › Seeds, fertilisers & pesticides](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md#seeds-fertilisers--pesticides).

#### `agri_molecules`
Purpose: the molecules a shop cannot sell freely. Platform data: no `business_id`, the same for every shop, changed only by migrations.
Key columns: `code` (primary key: lower-case letters and `_`), `name`, `status` (banned, restricted_use, crop_restricted), `restriction` (the rule in words), `source` (the order or list it comes from).
Rules: unique index `agri_molecules_name` (lower(name)); CHECK a restricted-use molecule has its rule; the source is not blank. Each row names its source. The rows: dicofol, dinocap and methomyl (banned, S.O. 4294(E) of 3 October 2023); dichlorvos, phorate and triazophos (banned for use, the list as on 31 March 2024); aluminium phosphide, methyl bromide, cypermethrin (its 3% smoke generator), DDT and carbofuran (restricted use); chlorpyrifos, dimethoate, malathion, mancozeb, oxyfluorfen, quinalphos and monocrotophos (barred on named crops, S.O. 4294(E)). The official list is longer; what is left to add is in [BACKLOG.md](BACKLOG.md).
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_crops`
Purpose: the crops and crop groups the cited orders name, which a bill's crop is matched to.
Key columns: `code` (primary key), `name` (unique, any case), `aliases` (TEXT[]: other names, such as jowar for sorghum).
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_molecule_crops`
Purpose: the crops a crop-restricted molecule must not be used on.
Key columns: PK (`molecule_code` -> `agri_molecules`, `crop_code` -> `agri_crops`).
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_farmers`
Purpose: a customer's details as a farmer. No Aadhaar number is kept.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `contact_id`), `village` (not blank), `relative_name` (father's or husband's name), `land_acres` (> 0, two places), `crops`, `farmer_id` (Agri Stack Farmer ID: letters, digits, `-` and `/`, 4-30), `notes`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules:
- Index `agri_farmers_village` (business, lower(village)); unique index `agri_farmers_farmer_id` (business, upper(Farmer ID)).
- Trigger `agri_farmers_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`): the contact is a customer.
- Trigger `agri_farmers_guard`: never deleted; the customer and who made it never change.
Added by: 1791962000000; 1791977000000 (`agri_farmers_guard` lets an erasure's change through: `business_privacy_erasing`).

#### `agri_bills`
Purpose: a sale's farm-input details: the season of a credit bill, or a walk-in farmer's name and village.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `document_id`), `season` (kharif, rabi, zaid), `season_year` (2000-2100), `buyer_name`, `village`, `created_by`, `created_at`. The season bill's due date is the document's own `due_on`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `business_documents`.
Rules:
- CHECK season and year together; at least a season, a name or a village.
- Index `agri_bills_season` (business, year, season) of season bills.
- Trigger `agri_bills_check` (before insert): the document is a sale; a season only on a named customer's sale; a name or village only on a walk-in's.
- Trigger `agri_bills_append_only` (function `agri_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_sale_lines`
Purpose: each fertiliser, seed, pesticide or bio-input line of a sale, as it was billed.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `line_id`), `document_id`, `sku_id`, `input_kind` (fertiliser, seed, pesticide, bio_input), `subsidised`, `pos_receipt` (letters, digits, `-` and `/`, 3-40), `crop` (as said), `crop_code` -> `agri_crops`, `molecules` (TEXT[], the item's then), `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `line_id`) -> `business_document_lines`; (`business_id`, `document_id`) -> `business_documents`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules:
- CHECK subsidised exactly when there is a PoS receipt; subsidised only for a fertiliser; a matched crop only with the crop said.
- Indexes `agri_sale_lines_document` and the partial `agri_sale_lines_subsidised` (business, item).
- Trigger `agri_sale_lines_check` (function `agri_sale_line_check`): never changed or deleted; on insert, the document is a sale, no molecule is banned, the matched crop is not barred for any molecule, a fertiliser, seed or pesticide line has a licence in force covering it at the sale's location that day, for wholesale sales when the buyer has a GSTIN and retail sales otherwise, while the business has `licences` on (`business_licence_covering`), and a seed line's lot is not past its validity.
Added by: 1791962000000; 1791979050000 (`agri_sale_line_check`: the licence for the kind of sale, and in force by a renewal applied for).

#### `agri_pos_checks`
Purpose: the PoS device's stock typed in at a location on a business day.
Key columns: `location_id`, `checked_on`, `notes`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `location_id`) -> `business_locations`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, request key); index `agri_pos_checks_day` (business, location, day); trigger `agri_pos_checks_append_only`: never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_pos_check_lines`
Purpose: one subsidised fertiliser of a PoS check: what the PoS device showed and the shop's stock at that moment.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `check_id`, `sku_id`), `pos_stock` (>= 0), `app_stock` (>= 0), both in the item's stock unit.
Relations: (`business_id`, `check_id`) -> `agri_pos_checks`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Rules: trigger `agri_pos_check_lines_append_only`: never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791962000000.

#### `agri_settings`
Purpose: a farm-input shop's setting: a pesticide not endorsed on the location's insecticide licence, or whose manufacturer has no principal certificate on it in force, is billed with a warning (warn) or refused (block). No row: warn.
Key columns: `business_id` (primary key), `endorsement_check` (warn, block), `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Added by: 1791979050000.

## Optical shops

An optical shop's own tables (type `optical`), added by 1791963000000 optical. Module `optical` ("Prescriptions & spectacle orders", optional, optical only, on) needs catalog, contacts and sales; permissions `optical.view`, `optical.create` (record and correct prescriptions; book spectacle orders), `optical.edit` (the lab and workshop steps, an order's details, delivery), `optical.approve` (the quality check), `optical.cancel` and `optical.print`. The role template `optometrist` is given to optical shops only (`business_role_templates.business_types`). Spectacle orders are numbered in the series kind `spectacle_order` (prefix `SPO`); the advance of a cancelled order is handed back as a `business_collection_refunds` row with `source` optical. A contact lens's powers are items of one product, each keeping `power`, `baseCurve` and `diameter` in `catalog_skus.attributes` (no table). How they are used: [BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md › Optical shops](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md#optical-shops).

#### `optical_settings`
Purpose: how many months after the last eye test a shop's customer is due for another.
Key columns: PK `business_id`, `recall_months` (1-60, default 12; 12 is used until the row is made), `updated_by`, `updated_at`.
Added by: 1791963000000.

#### `optical_prescriptions`
Purpose: a customer's eye prescription.
Key columns: `contact_id`, `examined_on`, `examiner_kind` (staff, outside), `examiner_user_id` (a staff examiner), `examiner_name` (as it was), `registration_no` (an outside examiner's), `pd_binocular` (40-85 mm) or `pd_right` and `pd_left` (20-45 mm each), `notes`, `valid_until`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules:
- CHECK a staff examiner names the user, and only a staff one does; an outside examiner has a registration number; the PD of both eyes or of each eye, never both, and each eye's together; valid until on or after the day of the test.
- UNIQUE (business, request key); indexes `optical_prescriptions_contact` (business, customer, newest test first) and `optical_prescriptions_period`.
- Trigger `optical_prescriptions_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`): the contact is a customer.
- Trigger `optical_prescriptions_guard`: never deleted; once a spectacle order uses it, it never changes (a correction is a new prescription); the request and who recorded it never change.
Added by: 1791963000000.

#### `optical_prescription_rows`
Purpose: a prescription's powers, one row per eye and vision.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `prescription_id`, `eye`, `zone`), `eye` (right, left), `zone` (distance, near, intermediate), `sph` and `cyl` (-30 to +30 in steps of 0.25), `axis` (1-180), `add_power` (+0.25 to +4.00 in steps of 0.25), `prism` (over 0, at most 20, in steps of 0.25) with `prism_base` (in, out, up, down), `acuity` (as written: 6/6, N6).
Relations: (`business_id`, `prescription_id`) -> `optical_prescriptions`.
Rules:
- CHECK a CYL only with an SPH; an AXIS exactly when the CYL is given and is not 0; no ADD on distance; a prism with its base; at least an SPH, an ADD, a prism or the acuity.
- Trigger `optical_prescription_rows_guard`: no row is added, changed or removed once a spectacle order uses the prescription.
Added by: 1791963000000.

#### `optical_orders`
Purpose: a spectacle order, the job card.
Key columns: `location_id`, `order_no` (series kind `spectacle_order`), `series_id`, `contact_id`, `customer_name` and `customer_phone` (as booked), `prescription_id`, `rx` (JSONB: the prescription and its rows as booked), `pd_right` and `pd_left` (20-45 mm), `fitting_height_right` and `fitting_height_left` (5-45 mm), `made_at` (in_house, lab), `lab_name`, `lab_order_no`, `promised_on`, `status` (booked, at_lab, in_workshop, quality_check, ready, delivered, cancelled), `remake_of`, `remake_reason` (power_change, non_adaptation, measurement, lab_defect, breakage, coating, other), `remake_cost_by` (shop, lab, customer), `remake_note`, `tax_mode`, `subtotal`, `tax`, `round_off` (between -1 and 1), `total`, `savings`, `discount_amount` or `discount_percent` with `discount_reason`, `notes`, `advance_collection_id`, `checked_by`/`checked_at` (the quality check last passed), `document_id` (the bill), `delivery_key`, `delivered_by`/`delivered_at`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_by`/`cancelled_at`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, ...) -> `business_locations`, `business_number_series`, `business_contacts`, `optical_prescriptions`, `optical_orders` (`remake_of`), `business_collections` (`advance_collection_id`) and `business_documents` (`document_id`).
Rules:
- CHECK total = subtotal + tax + round-off; a discount by amount or by percent, with its reason; a lab's name exactly when made at a lab, and a lab order number only then; a remake has its reason and who bears the cost; a remake the shop or the lab bears has a total of 0; ready or delivered only with the quality check stamped; the delivery's and the cancellation's columns exactly with their status, and a bill only once delivered.
- UNIQUE (business, order number), (business, request key), (business, bill) and (business, advance). Indexes `optical_orders_period`, `optical_orders_contact`, `optical_orders_prescription`, the partial `optical_orders_open` (business, promised day) and `optical_orders_remake`.
- Trigger `optical_orders_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`).
- Trigger `optical_orders_insert_check`: the prescription is the customer's; a new order is booked; a remake is of a delivered order of the same customer.
- Trigger `optical_orders_guard`: never deleted; a delivered or cancelled order never changes; an open one keeps its number, location, customer, prescription, prices, discount, remake and advance, and the request and who booked it; status moves booked → at_lab or in_workshop, at_lab and in_workshop to each other or to quality_check, quality_check → ready, at_lab or in_workshop, ready → at_lab, in_workshop or delivered, and any open status → cancelled; it becomes ready only when the same change stamps the quality check.
Added by: 1791963000000; 1791977000000 (`optical_orders_guard` lets an erasure's change through: `business_privacy_erasing`).

#### `optical_order_lines`
Purpose: an order's frame, lenses and other items, as booked.
Key columns: `order_id`, `part` (frame, lens, item), `eye` (a lens's: right, left), `sku_id`, `own_frame`, `description`; a lens's `lens_type` (single_vision, bifocal, progressive), `lens_index` (1.40-2.00), `material` (cr39, polycarbonate, trivex, glass, other), `coatings` (TEXT[]: anti_reflective, blue_cut, uv, photochromic, scratch_resistant), `tint` and `brand`; an item line's `label`, `unit`, `quantity`, `unit_price`, `amount`, `discount`, `tax_rate`, `net`, `tax`, `price_rule_id`, `entered_unit`, `entered_quantity`, `entered_unit_price`, `unit_factor`, `hsn_code`, `mrp` and `offers` (JSONB array).
Relations: (`business_id`, `order_id`) -> `optical_orders`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`; (`business_id`, `unit`) -> `business_units`; (`business_id`, `price_rule_id`) -> `business_price_rules`.
Rules:
- CHECK a lens has its eye and type, and only a lens has lens details; the customer's own frame has a description and no item; any other frame, and every other item line, names an item; `optical_order_lines_priced_with_item` (the priced columns all present with an item, none without); the discount at most the amount; `optical_order_lines_entered_all_or_none`.
- Unique indexes `optical_order_lines_one_frame` and `optical_order_lines_one_lens_an_eye`; index `optical_order_lines_order`.
- Trigger `optical_order_lines_guard`: a line goes in only while its order is booked, and is never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791963000000.

#### `optical_order_events`
Purpose: an order's history: every change of status, and every note with none.
Key columns: `order_id`, `from_status`, `to_status`, `note`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Rules: index `optical_order_events_order`; trigger `optical_order_events_append_only` (function `optical_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791963000000.

#### `optical_recall_reminders`
Purpose: each reminder given to a customer due for an eye test.
Key columns: `contact_id`, `channel` (whatsapp, call, sms), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Rules: index `optical_recall_reminders_contact`; trigger `optical_recall_reminders_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`); trigger `optical_recall_reminders_append_only` (function `optical_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791963000000.

## Vehicle service & auto parts

A garage's own tables (type `garage`), added by 1791964000000 garage. Module `garage` ("Vehicles & job cards", optional, garage only, on) needs catalog, contacts and sales; permissions `garage.view`, `garage.create` (add vehicles, open job cards), `garage.edit` (work on job cards), `garage.approve` (an odometer reading lower than the last), `garage.cancel` and `garage.print`. The role template `mechanic` is given to garages only (`business_role_templates.business_types`). Job cards are numbered in the series kind `job_card` (prefix `JC`); the advance of a cancelled job card is handed back as a `business_collection_refunds` row with `source` garage. A job card's bills are ordinary sales (`business_documents`): the customer's, and on a cashless claim the insurer's. How they are used: [BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md › Vehicle service & auto parts](BUSINESS_PLATFORM_DATA_MODEL.md#vehicle-service--auto-parts).

#### `garage_settings`
Purpose: a garage's months and kilometres to the next service after a job, and the item that bills the owner's share of a cashless claim.
Key columns: PK `business_id`, `service_months` (1-36, default 6), `service_km` (500-100,000, default 5,000), `claim_share_sku_id`, `updated_by`, `updated_at`. 6 months and 5,000 km are used until the row is made.
Relations: (`business_id`, `claim_share_sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_vehicles`
Purpose: a customer's vehicle.
Key columns: `registration_no` (`^[A-Z0-9]{4,15}$` with a letter and a digit), `make`, `model`, `variant`, `fuel` (petrol, diesel, cng, petrol_cng, lpg, electric, hybrid), `year` (1950-2100), `contact_id` (the owner now), `chassis_no` (5-30 letters and digits), `engine_no` (3-30), `odometer_km` and `odometer_at` (the latest reading; both or neither), `insurance_expires_on`, `puc_expires_on`, `next_service_on`, `next_service_km`, `notes`, `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules:
- UNIQUE (business, registration number); unique index `garage_vehicles_chassis` (business, chassis number, where given); index `garage_vehicles_owner`.
- Trigger `garage_vehicles_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`): the owner is a customer.
- Trigger `garage_vehicles_owner_history` (function `garage_vehicle_owner_history`): writes `garage_vehicle_owners` when a vehicle is added and whenever its owner changes.
- Trigger `garage_vehicles_guard`: never deleted (switched off instead); keeps who added it and when; its latest reading is only ever the last row of its odometer log.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_vehicle_owners`
Purpose: every owner a vehicle has had, from the moment they became its owner.
Key columns: `vehicle_id`, `contact_id`, `since`, `recorded_by`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `vehicle_id`) -> `garage_vehicles`; (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules: indexes `garage_vehicle_owners_vehicle` and `garage_vehicle_owners_contact`; trigger `garage_vehicle_owners_append_only` (function `garage_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_odometer_readings`
Purpose: a vehicle's odometer log.
Key columns: `vehicle_id`, `reading_km` (0-9,999,999), `source` (job_in, job_out, vehicle), `job_card_id` (exactly when read at a job card), `lower_approved_by` and `lower_reason` (together: the manager who approved a reading lower than the last, and why), `recorded_by`, `recorded_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `vehicle_id`) -> `garage_vehicles`; (`business_id`, `job_card_id`) -> `garage_job_cards`.
Rules:
- Index `garage_odometer_readings_vehicle`.
- Trigger `garage_odometer_readings_check` (function `garage_odometer_check`): never changed or deleted; a reading lower than the vehicle's last is refused without the approval, and only a lower reading carries one.
- Trigger `garage_odometer_readings_latest` (function `garage_odometer_latest`): the vehicle's `odometer_km` and `odometer_at` follow the new reading.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_job_cards`
Purpose: a job card: a vehicle's visit from its arrival to its delivery.
Key columns: `location_id`, `job_no` (series kind `job_card`), `series_id`, `vehicle_id`, `registration_no` and `vehicle_name` (as they were), `contact_id`, `customer_name` and `customer_phone` (as they were), `odometer_in`, `fuel_level` (empty, quarter, half, three_quarters, full), `complaints` (TEXT[], 1-20), `inspection_notes`, `bay`, `promised_at`, `status` (received, estimate_sent, approved, in_progress, waiting_parts, ready, delivered, cancelled), `estimate_version` (0 before the first), `approved_version`, `tax_mode`, `subtotal`, `tax`, `round_off`, `total`, `notes`, `advance_collection_id`, `document_id` (the customer's bill), `insurer_document_id` (the insurer's bill), `bill_key`, `next_service_on`, `next_service_km`, `delivered_by`/`delivered_at`, `cancel_reason`, `cancelled_by`/`cancelled_at`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, ...) -> `business_locations`, `business_number_series`, `garage_vehicles`, `business_contacts`, `business_collections` (`advance_collection_id`) and `business_documents` (`document_id`, `insurer_document_id`).
Rules:
- CHECK total = subtotal + tax + round-off (round-off under 1 either way); no total before the first version; the approved version not above the current one; estimate sent and approved only with an estimate, approved only with an approved version; delivered exactly with its delivery and bill key, and bills only when delivered; the next service only when delivered; cancelled exactly with its reason, who and when.
- UNIQUE (business, job number), (business, request key), (business, customer's bill), (business, insurer's bill) and (business, advance). Indexes `garage_job_cards_period`, `garage_job_cards_vehicle`, `garage_job_cards_contact` and the partial `garage_job_cards_open` (open job cards by status).
- Trigger `garage_job_cards_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`).
- Trigger `garage_job_cards_insert_check`: a new job card is received with no estimate, for a vehicle in use.
- Trigger `garage_job_cards_guard`: never deleted; a delivered or cancelled job card never changes; an open one keeps its number, location, vehicle, customer, odometer in, price terms, advance and request; the estimate goes up one version at a time and an approval is of the version it stands at; status moves received → estimate_sent, approved, in_progress, waiting_parts or ready; estimate_sent → approved; approved, in_progress and waiting_parts → estimate_sent, in_progress, waiting_parts or ready; ready → estimate_sent, in_progress, waiting_parts or delivered; any open → cancelled; approved only with the estimate as it stands approved; delivered with lines only with a bill.
Added by: 1791964000000; 1791977000000 (`garage_job_cards_guard` lets an erasure's change through: `business_privacy_erasing`).

#### `garage_job_card_mechanics`
Purpose: the members working on a job card.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `job_card_id`, `user_id`), `assigned_by`, `assigned_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `job_card_id`) -> `garage_job_cards`.
Rules: index `garage_job_card_mechanics_user`; trigger `garage_job_card_mechanics_guard` (function `garage_job_card_parts_guard`): changed only while the job card is open, and a mechanic is an active member of the business.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_job_card_lines`
Purpose: a job card's estimate as it stands.
Key columns: `job_card_id`, `kind` (part, labour, sublet), `payer` (customer, insurer), `sku_id`, `label`, `unit`, `quantity`, `unit_price` (the rate for one unit in the job card's price terms), `amount`, `tax_rate`, `net`, `tax`, `price_rule_id`, `hsn_code`, `mrp`, `description`, `vendor_name` and `vendor_bill_no` (outside work's workshop and its bill), `done`, `done_by`/`done_at`, `added_by`/`added_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `job_card_id`) -> `garage_job_cards`; (`business_id`, `sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`; (`business_id`, `unit`) -> `business_units`; (`business_id`, `price_rule_id`) -> `business_price_rules`.
Rules:
- CHECK a workshop's name exactly on outside work, and its bill number only there; net + tax = amount (GST included) or net = amount (before GST); done exactly with who and when.
- Index `garage_job_card_lines_job`.
- Trigger `garage_job_card_lines_guard` (function `garage_job_card_parts_guard`): changed only while the job card is open; keeps who added it and when; a part is goods and labour or outside work a service; a line is the insurer's only on a job card with a claim.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_claims`
Purpose: a job card's insurance claim.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `job_card_id`), `insurer_id`, `mode` (cashless, reimbursement), `policy_no`, `claim_no`, `surveyor_name`, `surveyor_phone`, `approved_amount` (> 0), `deductible`, `depreciation`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `job_card_id`) -> `garage_job_cards`; (`business_id`, `insurer_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules: index `garage_claims_insurer`; trigger `garage_claims_guard` (function `garage_job_card_parts_guard`): changed only while the job card is open; the insurer is a customer, not the job card's own; not removed while a line is the insurer's.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_estimate_versions`
Purpose: every version of a job card's estimate, as it was saved.
Key columns: `job_card_id`, `version` (1-999), `subtotal`, `tax`, `round_off`, `total`, `insurer_lines`, `lines` (JSONB array: the lines as they were), `source` (estimate, parts, claim), `note`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `job_card_id`) -> `garage_job_cards`.
Rules: CHECK total = subtotal + tax + round-off; UNIQUE (business, job card, version) and (business, request key); trigger `garage_estimate_versions_append_only` (function `garage_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_estimate_approvals`
Purpose: the owner's approval of a version of the estimate.
Key columns: `job_card_id`, `version`, `amount` (the version's total), `approved_by` (a name), `channel` (in_person, phone, whatsapp), `approved_at` (at most 5 minutes after it is recorded), `note`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `recorded_by`, `recorded_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `job_card_id`, `version`) -> `garage_estimate_versions`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, request key); index `garage_estimate_approvals_job`; trigger `garage_estimate_approvals_check`: the job card is open, the version is the one it stands at, and the amount is that version's total; trigger `garage_estimate_approvals_append_only` (function `garage_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_job_card_events`
Purpose: a job card's history: every change of status, and every note with none.
Key columns: `job_card_id`, `from_status`, `to_status`, `note`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Rules: index `garage_job_card_events_job`; trigger `garage_job_card_events_append_only` (function `garage_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791964000000.

#### `garage_reminders`
Purpose: each reminder given to a vehicle's owner.
Key columns: `vehicle_id`, `kind` (service, insurance, puc), `channel` (whatsapp, call, sms), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `vehicle_id`) -> `garage_vehicles`.
Rules: index `garage_reminders_vehicle`; trigger `garage_reminders_append_only` (function `garage_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791964000000.

## Gyms, studios & coaching

A gym's, studio's or coaching centre's own tables (type `memberships`), added by 1791965000000 memberships. Module `member_plans` ("Plans & memberships", optional, memberships only, on) needs catalog, contacts and sales; permissions `member_plans.view`, `member_plans.create` (check members in; sell and renew memberships), `member_plans.edit` (set up plans; freeze, transfer and upgrade memberships), `member_plans.approve` (approve what a cancellation gives back), `member_plans.cancel`, `member_plans.refund` and `member_plans.export`. The role template `trainer` is given to these businesses only (`business_role_templates.business_types`). Memberships are numbered in the series kind `membership` (prefix `MS`). A plan is sold as a service item of its own (`catalog_skus`); a membership's bill is an ordinary sale (`business_documents`), and what a cancellation or an upgrade gives back is an ordinary credit note (`business_returns`) with, for a cancellation, an ordinary refund (`business_refunds`). The trigger `business_return_lines_member_plan` (function `member_plan_line_return`) on `business_return_lines` refuses a return of a bill line that sold a membership (its plan's or its joining fee's) unless the return's request key is the membership's own `credit_key`.

#### `member_settings`
Purpose: when overdue fees turn a member away at check-in, and the last member code given out.
Key columns: PK `business_id`, `dues_block_days` (0-365, default 7; NULL: never), `last_member_no`, `updated_by`, `updated_at`. 7 days are used until the row is made (on first save, or with the first member code).
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_plan_types`
Purpose: a plan the business sells.
Key columns: `name` (unique index `member_plan_types_name` on the business and the name in any case), `kind` (duration, sessions, both), `duration_unit` (days, months) and `duration` (at most 3,660 days or 120 months), `sessions` (1-1,000), `price_sku_id`, `joining_sku_id`, `max_freeze_days` (0-365), `max_freezes` (0-12), `grace_days` (0-90), `transferable`, `policy_version`, `active`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `price_sku_id`) and (`business_id`, `joining_sku_id`) -> `catalog_skus`; (`business_id`, `id`, `policy_version`) -> `member_plan_policies` (deferrable, checked at the end of the transaction: `member_plan_types_policy_fkey`).
Rules:
- CHECK a duration and its unit exactly unless a pack of sessions; sessions exactly unless a plan by time; the joining fee item not the price item; freezes and freeze days both or neither, and none on a pack of sessions.
- UNIQUE (business, price item).
- Trigger `member_plan_types_check`: never deleted; keeps its price item, who made it and when; starts at policy version 1 and goes up one at a time; the price item is a service of the business and not a kit, and no other plan's joining fee; the joining fee is a service, not a kit, and no plan's price item.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_plan_policies`
Purpose: every version of a plan's refund policy, as it was written.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `plan_id`, `version`), `policy` (1-4,000 characters), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `plan_id`) -> `member_plan_types`.
Rules: trigger `member_plan_policies_append_only` (function `member_append_only`): never changed or deleted.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_profiles`
Purpose: a member's code, for their card and the desk.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `contact_id`), `member_code` (`^[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9-]{1,19}$`), `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `contact_id`) -> `business_contacts`.
Rules: UNIQUE (business, member code); trigger `member_profiles_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`): a member is a customer.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_memberships`
Purpose: a membership: a plan sold to a member, with its terms as sold.
Key columns: `location_id`, `membership_no` (series kind `membership`), `series_id`, `contact_id`, `plan_id`, `plan_name`, `kind`, `duration_unit`, `duration`, `sessions_total` (1-1,000), `sessions_used`, `price`, `price_net`, `price_tax`, `tax_rate`, `joining_fee`, `policy_version`, `max_freeze_days`, `max_freezes`, `grace_days`, `transferable`, `start_on`, `base_end_on`, `frozen_days`, `end_on`, `renewal_of`, `upgraded_from`, `transferred_from`, `document_id`, `line_id`, `joining_line_id`, `closed` (cancelled, transferred, upgraded), `closed_on`, `close_reason`, `closed_by`, `closed_at`, `close_key`, `close_hash`, `credit_key`, `return_id`, `credit` (> 0), `refund_id`, `refunded` (> 0), `approved_by`, `notes`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`/`created_at`, `updated_by`/`updated_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, ...) -> `business_locations`, `business_number_series`, `business_contacts`, `member_plan_types`, `member_plan_policies` (plan and version), `member_memberships` (`renewal_of`, `upgraded_from`, `transferred_from`), `business_documents`, `business_document_lines` (`line_id`, `joining_line_id`) and `business_returns`; `refund_id` -> `business_refunds`.
Rules:
- CHECK a duration exactly unless a pack of sessions, and sessions exactly unless a plan by time; sessions used not above the sessions; price = net + GST; a last day exactly unless a pack of sessions, not before the first day, and `end_on` = `base_end_on` + `frozen_days`; at most one of renewal, upgrade and transfer; a joining fee only with its line; closed exactly with its last day, reason, who, when and request (key and hash); the last day counted not before the day before the first day; a credit key only on a cancellation or an upgrade, a credit note only with it and with its value, a refund only on a cancelled membership with a credit note and with its amount, an approver only on a cancellation.
- UNIQUE (business, membership number) and (business, request key); unique indexes `member_memberships_line` (one membership per bill line, transfers apart), `member_memberships_upgraded_from` and `member_memberships_transferred_from`; indexes `member_memberships_member`, `member_memberships_period`, the partial `member_memberships_running` (not closed, by last day), `member_memberships_plan`, `member_memberships_document` and `member_memberships_joining_line`.
- Trigger `member_memberships_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`).
- Trigger `member_memberships_insert_check`: a new membership is running with nothing used, frozen or given back; it is on a line of its plan's price item on the bill named, a joining fee on the same bill; a sale's bill is the member's own sale; a transfer carries on a membership closed as transferred, on its bill, line and plan, to another member; a renewal or an upgrade is of the member's own membership, an upgrade of one closed as upgraded.
- Trigger `member_memberships_guard`: never deleted; keeps its member, plan, terms, start, last day as sold, bill and request; its frozen days only follow its freezes; its sessions used never go down; once closed it changes no more, but its credit note and refund are written once; nothing is given back while it runs.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_freezes`
Purpose: each freeze of a membership.
Key columns: `membership_id`, `starts_on`, `planned_days` (1-365), `days` (0 to planned), `reason`, `ended_on`, `ended_by`, `ended_at`, `request_key`, `request_hash`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `membership_id`) -> `member_memberships`.
Rules:
- CHECK the early end exactly with who and when; days as planned until ended early, then the days from its start to the day it ended (0 when ended before it began).
- UNIQUE (business, request key); index `member_freezes_membership`.
- Trigger `member_freezes_check`: never deleted; only of a membership not closed, with a last day; a new freeze starts with its planned days, today (the business's date) or later, between the membership's first and last day, overlaps no other, and keeps within its freezes and days allowed (both as sold); ending early keeps its start, planned days and request, happens once, on the day it is recorded.
- Trigger `member_freezes_extend`: the membership's `frozen_days` and `end_on` follow its freezes.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_instalments`
Purpose: a membership's fee in instalments.
Key columns: PK (`business_id`, `membership_id`, `seq` (1-36)), `due_on`, `amount` (> 0).
Relations: (`business_id`, `membership_id`) -> `member_memberships`.
Rules: index `member_instalments_due`; trigger `member_instalments_append_only` (function `member_append_only`): never changed or deleted; constraint trigger `member_instalments_check` (deferred to the end of the transaction): written with its membership (not a transfer's), two or more numbered from 1, each due after the one before, adding up to the membership's bill.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_checkins`
Purpose: every check-in at the desk.
Key columns: `location_id`, `contact_id`, `membership_id`, `day` (the business's date of `created_at`, set by the database), `found_by` (search, code), `allowed`, `reason` (no_membership, not_started, expired, frozen, no_sessions, dues), `note`, `session_used`, `request_key`, `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, ...) -> `business_locations`, `business_contacts` and `member_memberships`.
Rules:
- CHECK a reason exactly when turned away; a session used only when let in; a membership when let in; no membership exactly when the reason is no_membership.
- UNIQUE (business, request key); unique index `member_checkins_one_session_a_day` (one session used a membership a day); indexes `member_checkins_day` and `member_checkins_member`.
- Trigger `member_checkins_customer` (function `business_contact_is_customer`); trigger `member_checkins_append_only` (function `member_append_only`).
- Trigger `member_checkins_check`: sets the day; the membership is the member's own; a session is counted only on a pack; letting in needs a membership not closed, started, within its last day and grace days, not frozen that day, and a session left when one is used.
- Trigger `member_checkins_session`: a session used is added to the membership's `sessions_used`.
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_events`
Purpose: everything that happened to a membership.
Key columns: `membership_id`, `kind` (sold, renewal, upgrade, transfer, frozen, freeze_ended, transferred, upgraded, cancelled), `note`, `details` (JSONB object), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `membership_id`) -> `member_memberships`.
Rules: index `member_events_membership`; trigger `member_events_append_only` (function `member_append_only`).
Added by: 1791965000000.

#### `member_reminders`
Purpose: each renewal or fees reminder given about a membership.
Key columns: `membership_id`, `kind` (renewal, fees), `channel` (whatsapp, call, sms), `created_by`, `created_at`.
Relations: (`business_id`, `membership_id`) -> `member_memberships`.
Rules: index `member_reminders_membership`; trigger `member_reminders_append_only` (function `member_append_only`).
Added by: 1791965000000.

## Laundry & dry cleaning

A laundry's and dry cleaner's own tables (type `laundry`), added by 1791966000000 laundry.
